- **1914.11** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-coat-is-just-a-coat` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer把Klara抱起来，走到衣钩下面，让孩子伸手去抓那件旧大衣。粗呢的袖口擦过掌心，Klara先愣住，随后皱着鼻子哭了一声。Josef Bauer笑着把大衣掀开，说“看，只有衣服”，灯影也跟着晃了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +learned_the_coat_rack_

- **1915.01** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury

  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000419:child_

  第四天，Elisabeth Berg的回信从门缝底下塞进来，纸边被门槛磨毛了。Elisabeth Berg约Klara星期六早上在街角面包店门口见，说想买一个两赫勒的小圆面包，两个人分。Mitzi在楼道里撞见Klara读信，笑着问是不是好消息；Klara把信折回四折，塞进内袋贴着胸口。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06; role_

  _effects: education higher; cash 6 (book allowance from the school fund); role; flag +works_evenings_for_school; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

  Klara和Helene Weiss 2合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; household; mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; flag +funeral_society_left_

  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1951.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Emil Hofer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Klara把床单对折，腾出一半，Emil Hofer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003654:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.03** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Klara下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1951.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara没有立刻答话，先去敲Gustav Kral的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Gustav Kral提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Klara把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-136; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1952.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/clean-audit` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Grete Bauer 2带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Klara把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Grete Bauer 2抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; organization_funds; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +passed_audit; knowledge “审计抽查了十二份合同，每一份都能对上签字人。”_

  Klara拿着锡罐在市场和厂门口转了一天。肉铺老板放了两枚克朗，面包房给了一袋隔夜面包，Katharina Kral把准备交房租的钱数出三分之一。天黑时锡罐里有四十多克朗，Klara把每一笔都写在包装纸上，连半赫勒也没漏。
  _effects: cash -2 (罢工第七天的汤锅); food; flag +work13_strike_fund_collected; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; organization_funds_

  洗衣房里蒸汽弥漫，熨斗压在衬衫领子上咝咝作响。老板娘翻了翻Klara熨好的第一件，说领口还差些火候，手上倒还稳。两打熨完，三个克朗放在围裙口袋里，铜板还带着体温。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

  Klara让Mitzi睡厨房的行军床，讲好每月三克朗饭钱，煤钱另算。白天把铺盖卷起来靠在碗柜边，晚上再铺开；厨房多一个人，面包和煤都下去得快，伙食上短了两克朗。看门人再来问时，Klara把藤箱指过去，说人住下了，灯油钱也记在账上。
  _effects: cash 3 (表亲交来的饭钱); relation kin with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; flag +kin_staying_over_

  Klara走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个（其中0.08克朗记入债务）)_

  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Klara胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。

  Klara喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Friedrich 2在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Berta Hofer把会费账本推回去，说外头那个组织答应给煤，只要名单。Klara把账本合上，用一块旧布包好塞进缝纫机底下，说先看看谁在往外递话。两人都没再提这事。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003926:child; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_district_list_private_

  水龙头滴滴答答地漏，两人都伸手去扶桶沿。Klara看见自家未洗的床单还堆着，胸口发闷，把桶让了半掌宽。晾绳上她的衬衣角被风掀起，正挡着邻家的窗户。

### 306 subject events

- **1914.08** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/wet-floor` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Alois Lechner端盆出去倒水，灶边淌了一地。Mitzi在摇床里翻身，摇床被湿地板滑得挪了位，撞在桌脚上。Alois Lechner回来时孩子哭得满脸通红，摇床腿松了一条。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (摇床撞上桌脚，受了惊); cash -0.3 (请楼下木匠紧摇床腿); mood -0.03_

- **1917.11** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Alois Lechner，楼里的Maria Berg正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Alois Lechner把裹着布条的Mitzi往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-neighbor-speaks` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  掌柜不肯赊，Emil Weiss从队尾挤过来，说这户人家的男人在印刷所做了十年，欠不了一筐煤。掌柜把“两赫勒”写在门牌号旁边，煤只装了小半筐。Mitzi抱着筐经过Emil Weiss身边，Emil Weiss替Mitzi把绳结紧了紧。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Mitzi Huber从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Mitzi。Mitzi把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1919.09** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  医院走廊里全是湿鞋印。Alois Lechner把Mitzi放在长椅上，自己去窗口交钱，六克朗换来一张薄薄的收据。护士把Mitzi领进观察室，白灯照得人睁不开眼。Mitzi抓着Alois Lechner的袖口不放，直到药水推进胳膊才松手。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.04_

  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Mitzi后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Mitzi没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Mitzi用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.030000000000000006; skill_

- **1920.11** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  洗衣房里全是蒸汽和碱水味。Mitzi靠在墙边等Alois Lechner，湿衣服堆在脚边。夜里Mitzi开始发烧，Alois Lechner把湿布叠成长条，敷在额头，凉了再换。三天后热度退下去，Mitzi的嘴唇起了一层白皮，Alois Lechner的手指也裂了口子。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Mitzi站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Emil Weiss在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Mitzi掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Mitzi才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  土豆滚进墙角的煤灰里，Mitzi弯腰去追，再抬头时Anna Lechner已经坐在井台边，裤腿湿了一片，哭声一抽一抽。Otto Weiss把孩子抱进灶间，Mitzi跟在后面，手指抠着门框。Alois Lechner回来没问经过，只把Mitzi的碗收走一半，说“看个人都看不住”。
  _effects: health -0.03 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.060000000000000026; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1922.09** `r2-leisure.swimming-lesson::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/nearly-drowned` tags: leisure, swimming, river, fear, lesson, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Mitzi刚把脚抬起来，水就没过下巴。河底的石头一滑，整个人往下沉，眼前全是绿色的光。Friedrich Hofer 2一把抓住Mitzi的后领，把人拖到浅水；Mitzi吐出一口水，咳得眼泪都出来。Rosa在岸上喊，Alois Lechner已经跑进水里，鞋都没脱。
  _effects: mood -0.15; condition; flag +nearly_drowned_in_river; relation guardian with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1922.10** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Mitzi在土豆摊前蹲了很久，挑了一磅没有芽眼的。付完钱，手里只剩几个铜板，又去买了盐。围裙兜太浅，Mitzi用下巴压住布边，走两步就低头看一眼。到家Alois Lechner把土豆倒进盆里，盐包放在灶台边。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销（其中0.15克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-neighbor-speaks` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “记我名下。”Sigmund Freud说完，掌柜才把秤砣挪开。账本上多出两赫勒的利息，煤筐却只装了半下。Mitzi跟着Sigmund Freud走出门，Sigmund Freud低声嘱咐明天先把钱送来，别让掌柜再找到话头。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-sigmund-freud_

- **1923.03** `r2-rural.village-school::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/learned-the-letters` tags: rural, school, childhood, teacher, literacy, agriculture, small_town, peace
  Mitzi把石板放在膝盖上，Friedrich Hofer 2用粉笔在木板上写字母。教室里只有一排课桌，后排的孩子坐在倒扣的奶桶上。下课铃是一只旧牛铃，Friedrich Hofer 2摇的时候，窗外的羊也跟着叫。
  _effects: flag +learned_letters_at_village_school; role; skill; education primary_

- **1923.12** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Grete Stein从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Mitzi答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Mitzi回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Grete Stein看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +wish:knowledge:pursuing_

- **1924.01** `task-vienna-poor-apprentice.foreman-introduction/foreman-records-name` tags: adolescence, apprentice, work, education, poverty, printshop, task, task:work
  工头说暂时没有空缺，但答应把他的名字记在招工簿上。亲戚在旁边补了两句好话，工头只点点头；他走出来时，招工簿还摊在门房的木台上。
  _effects: flag +name_in_hiring_book; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1925.01** `kernel-edu.prepare-for-exam::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-prepare-barely` tags: task, adolescence, school, exam, study, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Mitzi白天帮家里干活，只能夜里复习。煤油灯熏得眼睛发酸，第二天考试时，Mitzi把会做的题做完，剩下的凭记忆填满。成绩不高，但够升级。Anton Weiss在成绩单上写：尚有潜力。
  _effects: skill; condition; reputation 0.01; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; flag +passed_exam_barely; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/factory-hand` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Mitzi分到装订间，每天站十个小时，工钱按件算。第一周结束，Mitzi的指关节肿了，工头说习惯就好。
  _effects: employment Buchbinderhilfe @ org-winter-print; condition_

- **1926.01** `task-vienna-poor-apprentice.foreman-introduction/relative-refuses-guarantee` tags: adolescence, apprentice, work, education, poverty, printshop, task, task:work
  亲戚临时反悔，不肯为一个刚离开旧作坊的学徒作保，说自己在厂里还要做人。他站在门口，手里攥着熨平的衬衫袖口，把路上想好的话又咽回去。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1926.10** `vienna-adolescence.stolen-roll/share-the-roll` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Mitzi蹲在面包房后门的台阶上，手指抠着筐沿。Grete Lechner提着篮子过来，脚步很轻，掰下半个黑面包塞进Mitzi手里，说“别让里面的人看见”。Mitzi咬了两口，把剩下的包进纸里，回家放在桌上。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1927.01** `p10-edu-thresholds.realschule-scholarship/grant-missed` tags: adolescence, education, school, exam, apprenticeship, work, transition, scholarship
  第二次去房东家时，楼道里正在烧煤，气味呛人。房东太太翻了半天围裙口袋，只找出半张空白纸条。Mitzi一路小跑到学校，台阶上的影子已经拉得很长，窗口的木板正在往下放。
  _effects: mood -0.15999999999999998; cash -0.6 (两天来回的电车钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); knowledge “基金会减免申请缺一份房东租金收据，窗口只在星期三和星期五开”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.job-or-school/negotiated-half-week` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用铅笔尖戳着排班表上星期四那一格，说缺的活计要他自己找时间补。Mitzi点头，把课程表折成小方块放进工装口袋。
  _effects: employment 印刷厂杂工 @ org-winter-print; cash 15 (每周工钱减少后的收入); education apprenticeship; relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/joins-union` tags: adolescence, labour, union
  工会的人在厂门口拦住Mitzi，说学徒工也该有工票和保险。Mitzi每月交一克朗会费，把会员证塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: role; cash -1 (union dues（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.leave-bad-workshop/half-measures-stay` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Mitzi把窗户指给Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne看，说木板钉死了，味道散不出去。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让伙计拆掉一半，窗缝里透进风，油墨味淡了一点。清洗剂还是原来那桶，Mitzi每天下午太阳穴发紧，就用冷水搓手指。工位保住了，日薪照发，工钱袋和平时一样沉。
  _effects: health 0.019999999999999997 (窗户拆掉一半后气味稍减); condition; relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; cash 1 (保住工位，照常领日薪); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1929.01** `p10-edu-thresholds.night-school-fee/enrolled-absent` tags: adolescence, education, school, exam, apprenticeship, work, transition, night_school
  报名处的走廊里摆着几把椅子，他是倒数第二个交钱的。Grete Stein把课表折好递过来，说缺课超过三次要重报。图钉扎进墙面时，床板在下面轻轻响了一声。
  _effects: cash -6 (夜校一学期学费（其中3克朗记入债务）); education apprenticeship; mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.job-or-school/negotiated-half-week` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用铅笔尖戳着排班表上星期四那一格，说缺的活计要他自己找时间补。Mitzi点头，把课程表折成小方块放进工装口袋。
  _effects: employment 印刷厂杂工 @ org-winter-print; cash 15 (每周工钱减少后的收入); education apprenticeship; relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1930.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/semmel-no-words` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  在柜台前买了两只 Semmel，递过一只，自己咬另一只，旧账一个字没提。对方接过面包，两人并肩站在店门口，把那点热气吃完。钱的事还横在中间，但对方说星期六照旧来喝咖啡，别又躲着。
  _effects: cash -0.14 (两只 Semmel); food; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1930.05** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.rival-at-table/rival-leave-flowers` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Mitzi把带来的那束便宜紫罗兰搁在门边的小桌上，说工坊还有活，先走了。出门时正碰见Friedrich Stein在院子里擦自行车，两人客气地让了让路，谁也没先开口。紫罗兰用报纸裹着，花头朝着屋里；第二天Mitzi没有再登门，Ferdinand Weiss也没有来问。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1930.06** `vienna-printshop-daily.union-pamphlet/pamphlet-print-secretly` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Franz Lechner趁夜班把一版工会呼吁加薪的传单塞进Mitzi的排字盘里，说是替工友印的，谁都不会知道。Mitzi把那张纸压到排版最底层，机器照样轰隆转。
  _effects: flag +printed_union_pamphlet; relation colleague with person-franz-lechner; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi看Grete Stein盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Grete Stein愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Mitzi门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Mitzi打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-grete-stein; food_

- **1930.10** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Maria Berg没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Mitzi听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1931.01** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Anna Bauer在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Mitzi咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Mitzi手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Maria Berg等在门口，问值不值，Mitzi只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.02; skill_

- **1931.04** `p9-history-union-political.split/split-open-debate` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Mitzi把两派的名单都推到桌子中间，说先别表决，把分歧印出来公开辩论，会费和会员证经不起一次分裂。Franz Lechner皱眉想了半天，最后同意下周再议，会费簿子当场传了一圈。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-franz-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1931.05** `vienna-adult-social.wage-loan/wage-loan.refused` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Mitzi在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的走廊里拦住Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne，话还没说完，对方就摆手说账上没这笔规矩。Mitzi回车间时，工头正往布告栏上钉新的计件单。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.05_

- **1931.08** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-borrow-from-neighbor` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  看门人正把垃圾桶往院角推，见你回来，眼神在你和门牌之间转了一圈。房东收下四克朗，在账本上划掉一行，没提换锁的事。你上楼时腿有点发软，楼梯扶手凉得扎手。
  _effects: cash 4 (向邻居借来补交欠租); relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002475_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Mitzi在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Mitzi的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.16999999999999998 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Emil Hofer。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Alois Hofer把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Mitzi躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:70_

  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

  Emil Huber在班前会上说线不能停，Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung就让两头兼。他跑了一个月，靴底磨薄了一层。晚上回到家，图纸上的尺寸还在眼前浮。
  _effects: cash 3 (兼做新车间的图纸活，月底补贴); health -0.03 (两头跑，站立与乘车时间过长); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1932.02** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Ferdinand Winter却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Mitzi把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1932.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Mitzi胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-depression/leaves-vienna` tags: adulthood, depression, work, migration, unemployment
  Mitzi听说下奥地利的砖厂还招人，收拾了一只箱子，坐早班火车离开维也纳。厂里的工头在背后说，走了就别回来。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; unemployment (left the Vienna factory); cash -8 (train ticket and lodgings)_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Mitzi难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Berta Hofer；Mitzi一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Alois Hofer把钟表送进当铺。Mitzi落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:77_

- **1933.01** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +watch_pawned_

- **1933.01** `p10-migration-tasks.ask-travel-money/uncle-refuse-introduce-carrier` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, loan, relative
  舅父听完摇了摇头，说钱不借，倒可以给他写张条子，介绍一个跑布吕恩的车夫，让他搭便车走。舅母把点心收进橱里，说路上总得带点吃的。他收了条子，坐车夫的车走了两天。
  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; mood -0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (路上给车夫买烟叶的道谢钱)_

- **1933.10** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.send-out` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Mitzi转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Maria Berg还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Rosa Hofer。接生的人收六克朗，Alois Hofer在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Mitzi起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:113_

  Helene Winter每周来收二十赫勒，Mitzi把硬币从盐罐里倒出来，数够才让Helene Winter在卡上打孔。Friedrich Lechner说Mitzi的咳嗽不像能拖到夏天，Helene Winter听见了，把保单往包里塞得快了一点。
  _effects: cash -3 (每周二十赫勒的死亡（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; knowledge “the agent heard the doctor’s prognosis”_

  Mitzi把Berta Hofer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Berta Hofer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Mitzi听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  话在喉咙里滚了几遍，终究没有等到对方进门。门一响，有人就把紫罗兰放在门槛边，转身往街角走。花被早晨的扫帚拨到墙根，对方捡起来闻了闻，追到巷口只看见一个拐弯的背影，午后托人捎来一句话，说钱的事再算。
  _effects: cash -0.24 (一束紫罗兰（其中0.24克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  Mitzi把钟背板卸下来，发现一根发条断了。她从缝纫盒里找出一截细铁丝，把断头缠住，钟摆晃了几下又走了起来。走是走，每天慢一刻钟，她索性把指针往前拨了拨，凑合着看。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  _effects: flag +carried_choir_roll_home; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; organization_membership; knowledge “plainclothes men attend the choir rehearsals”_

  Mitzi赢了一叠面包券，Franz Stein伸手来收牌，手背上全是老年斑。Mitzi把券推回去一半，说够我吃到星期五。Franz Stein没有推辞，只把牌洗得更慢。Lena在门口说，这种牌局，赢了也像输了。
  _effects: cash 3 (塔罗克牌局的第四个人); mood 0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.03; flag +shared_winnings_with_old_player; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the veteran has been playing for bread since the factory clo”_

- **1939.05** `r13-turn.adoption::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/official-refused` tags: family, adoption, child, household, law, vienna, urban, crisis
  Ferdinand Weiss 2把申请推回来，说收养人必须有固定住所和两份担保，Mitzi的租房合同只有半年。Mitzi问能不能让熟人作保，Ferdinand Weiss 2说担保人自己也得有房产。Luise Winter在门口等着，手里攥着Mitzi给的一小块糖，糖纸已经捏皱。
  _effects: cash -2 (户籍簿上添的一行); condition; flag +adoption_refused; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003922:child; knowledge “adoption requires stable housing and two property-owning gua”_

  Mitzi把旧账本摊在汤锅旁边，翻到药费那一页，说今天得当场算清才肯坐下。Mitzi Lechner的勺子停在半空，最后也把本子拉过去，一行行对起数来。汤在炉子上咕嘟着，谁也没先盛；等账算到最后一个赫勒，Mitzi在页脚写下“结清”，两人才同时伸手去够勺子。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +medicine_bill_settled_

  圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。

  历史进程《圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。

- **1940.04** `r13-turn.addiction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sought-the-doctor` tags: health, addiction, alcohol, family, work, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi在上班时手抖得拿不住锉刀，被工头送去门诊。医生说再喝下去肝会坏，给了一张戒酒所的地址。Mitzi去了，门口排队的人比想象中多。轮到时，Mitzi说自己只是睡不着，医生说，那就先从今晚不喝开始。
  _effects: cash -3 (酒馆后门的第二瓶（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +sought_help_for_drinking; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; knowledge “the clinic offers a supervised withdrawal programme”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1940.07** `r2-law.eviction-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/repair-order` tags: law, eviction, housing, court, tenement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi没有争欠租，而是把一叠照片摊在桌上：天花板的裂缝、发霉的墙、冬天结冰的窗。Alois Hofer问Berta Berg修过没有，Berta Berg说修过；Mitzi拿出三封挂号信，都是催修的回执。Alois Hofer判Berta Berg先修房，欠租暂缓，修完再开庭。Berta Weiss在走廊里说，这招比哭有用。
  _effects: cash -6 (驱逐听证的十五分钟（其中6克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +court_ordered_repairs_before_eviction; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; knowledge “three registered repair requests were ignored”_

- **1940.08** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/collect-the-benefit` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  邻居出主意说，死者家属可以领一笔丧葬补助。Mitzi让Berta Lechner冒充远房表姐，自己在旁边按了手印；钱到手后，两人在厨房里分成三份，第三份留着买煤。第二个星期，Josef Winter寄来一封信，说抚恤金已经核准，请家属来拍一张遗像；Mitzi把信压在盐罐下面，盐受潮结了块。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒领的丧葬补助分成); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:296; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001000:child; condition; flag +collected_own_funeral_benefit; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1941.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/clean-audit` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Ferdinand Weiss 2带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Mitzi把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Ferdinand Weiss 2抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; organization_funds; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +passed_audit; knowledge “审计抽查了十二份合同，每一份都能对上签字人。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1941.01** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Mitzi刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Mitzi站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 52; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007608_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Mitzi的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Mitzi收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Mitzi先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 42; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 62; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.01** `p9-history-public-action.speak-meeting/motion-carried` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  后屋的钟停过一次，Mitzi 停顿时正好听见外面电车铃响。等说到“加薪”，Helene Weiss 3 先拍了桌子，随后整屋人跟上，灯罩里的火苗抖了抖。
  _effects: reputation 0.08000000000000002; skill; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1941.05** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.look-for-cheaper-bed` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi没去事务所，只在洗衣房打听更便宜的床位。有人提到利奥波德城另一条街上有一间合住的屋子，一个月少一克朗半，只是要跟三个人共用一间。Mitzi去看了两趟，量了窗子和床的位置，回来把箱子底下的旧报纸铺开算账。
  _effects: flag +looking_for_cheaper_housing; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1941.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  护士把冰袋按在Mitzi额上，水顺着鬓角流进衣领。Grete Stein坐在床脚，数着窗外渐渐亮起的铺面门板，说回去给炉子添块煤。隔壁床的老头整夜在喊一个女人的名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-grete-stein_

  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Mitzi把照片铁盒交给Alois Hofer抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销)_

- **1942.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::child-care-war/hospital-turned-away` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, vienna, urban
  Mitzi抱着Otto Hofer去医院，走廊里躺满缠绷带的士兵。挂号的人看了一眼孩子的脸，说病床先给伤员。Mitzi在门口站到天黑，最后用外套把孩子裹紧，走回家去；路上孩子问，医院为什么不收小孩。
  _effects: cash -1 (第三遍换湿布（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +hospital_refused_child; knowledge “the hospital reserves beds for wounded soldiers”; condition_

- **1942.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi Lechner 2嫌Mitzi的床单滴水滴到Mitzi Lechner 2家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Mitzi下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Mitzi把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Mitzi Lechner 2那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

- **1942.06** `oj18-cinema.reel::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-union/cut-the-reel` tags: cinema, film, projection, night, censorship, work, odd_job, vienna
  放映到第十一本，胶片在片门里卷成一团。Mitzi剪掉烧焦的一截，发现后面接的是一卷没送审的新闻片：军靴踏过广场，镜头扫过一张张被按下去的脸。Josef Huber在黑暗里说“接回正片”，Mitzi照做了。
  _effects: organization_found 小巷电影院 (household_firm); position 放映员 @cinema:person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:008140; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:346; cash 1 (projectionist wages); skill; flag +cut_the_reel; knowledge “第十一本胶片里夹着没送审的新闻片，{projectionist}让我接回正片。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1942.12** `r2-rural.hailstorm::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/repaired-the-roof` tags: rural, weather, neighbor, repair, vineyard, vienna, urban, crisis
  雹停后，屋顶漏了三处。Mitzi爬上梯子铺油毡，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在檐口。Alois Lechner在下面扶着梯子骂了两句，又去烧热水。修屋顶的木板和钉子花了九克朗，账记在欠条上。
  _effects: cash -9 (五月的冰雹（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +repaired_hail_damage; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  两年过去，Mitzi已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Mitzi也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 40; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 18 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1943.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.ask-fare` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Mitzi向Luise Weiss问清下个月郊外演唱会的车费由谁出，Luise Weiss说团里只贴一半，另一半要团员自己凑。Mitzi把四十赫勒硬币在指间转了两圈，最终没有放进收款盒，说等发了工钱再来；这一来一去，也算清了半票、酒棚和车钱各是多少。出门时Katharina Hofer正扛着啤酒桶进后厅，两人侧身让过。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000124:child; skill_

  Mitzi觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Otto Berg在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1943.08** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/take-in-a-lodger` tags: housing, home, health, family
  Mitzi把厨房角落隔出一块，租给一个在附近上工的学徒。每月多四克朗，但煤、水和厕所都要排队；夜里有人咳嗽，Mitzi睡不踏实，第二天眼下发青，孩子也在床上翻来覆去，四克朗换来一屋子醒着的人。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); condition_

- **1943.09** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Alois Lechner却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Mitzi把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.12** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Mitzi端着一只旧锅去敲Grete Stein的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Grete Stein让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Mitzi连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/motion-carries` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  投票结果是十九比十七。Mitzi把印章、钥匙和一本写满批注的账本放进纸箱，最后一圈走廊走得比平时慢。Alois Hofer在门口等着接手，手里已经拿着新的编制表；表上第一个被划掉的名字是Mitzi的秘书。
  _effects: position_end 入殓工 @mortuary:person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005554; reputation -0.04999999999999999; mood -0.07999999999999996; condition; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; flag +removed_by_vote; knowledge “新编制表第一个划掉的是我的秘书。”_

- **1944.06** `r3-mig.transit-camp::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/registered-by-nationality` tags: migration, refugees, camp, family, papers, vienna, urban, crisis
  Grete Stein按国籍把人分成三队，Mitzi被推到“无国籍”那一队。登记卡上只写了姓、年龄和一双鞋的尺码。第三排的草垫已经被人占了，Josef Huber挪出半个位置，说“夜里冷，别脱鞋”。
  _effects: food; condition; flag +registered_in_transit_camp; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; knowledge “the camp sorts people by nationality, not by family”_

- **1944.08** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; reputation -0.03; mood -0.02000000000000013_

- **1944.12** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Mitzi把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Luise Lechner放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Mitzi Lechner 3在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Mitzi躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Mitzi去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

- **1945.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/favor-refused` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Berta Weiss把一篮鸡蛋放在Mitzi家门口，说只要一个不用打卡的位子。Mitzi把鸡蛋拎回对方手里，说编制表不是自家的抽屉。两个人站在楼梯间，谁也没有再提这件事；第二天见面，还是点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; reputation 0.03; flag +refused_private_favor; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.01** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi数出八赫勒交给Franz Lechner，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Franz Lechner说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Mitzi盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; flag +funeral_society_member_

  四楼的男人把门开了一条缝，说他弟弟还在战俘营，谁能让弟弟回来就投谁。Mitzi答不上来，只能把传单折小塞进门缝。下楼时，Mitzi听见他把传单撕了，纸屑从楼梯缝里飘下来。

  你把已经掏出来的赫勒又放回口袋，说不赊了。老板哼了一声，把账本往柜台上一合。你拎着空筐跟邻居往火车站方向走，在货运站边的煤渣堆里捡了半袋碎煤和焦炭。手指冻得发僵，指甲缝里全是黑灰。

- **1948.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-seal-was-used` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Maria Kral 2用Mitzi留在抽屉里的印章签了一份采购单，把库房里最后一批煤卖给了城外的砖厂。Mitzi发现时，账已经做平，签字栏里是Mitzi自己的名字。Maria Kral 2说只是替Mitzi省了麻烦，还把那支用旧的印泥放回了原处。
  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱（其中3克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.1; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand; mood -0.07000000000000006; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123_

  战后新的名单贴在市政厅墙上，Mitzi从头看到尾，没有找到Alois Hofer。旁边有人哭着从人群里挤出来，Mitzi让开一步，又回头看了一眼那片墙。回家后，Mitzi把Alois Hofer的照片从抽屉里拿出来，摆到桌上。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +checked_the_postwar_list; role; knowledge “the partner is absent from the postwar casualty list”_

- **1948.01** `r13-work.reference-letter::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/letter-refused` tags: work, reference, hiring, favour, reputation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Winter说Mitzi走的时候没有打招呼，现在写信等于打自己的脸。Mitzi站在门房外，把来意又说了一遍，Josef Winter低头去拨炉子，不再接话。回家的路上，Mitzi把准备好的一小包烟叶捏碎了，撒在路边的雪里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +work13_reference_letter_refused; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

  Mitzi把Berta Berg的名字写在“联络员”后面，月薪一克朗，职责是每周来两次、把外面的消息带进来。Franz Lechner说这个岗位没有编制，Mitzi说先按临时用工算；第一个月，Berta Berg带来的消息比文件还多。
  _effects: position 联络员 @mortuary:person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005554; organization_funds; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; flag +appointed_ally; knowledge “联络员不在正式编制里，工资走临时用工。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: position 顾问 @mortuary:person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005554; organization_funds; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; knowledge “顾问岗位没有固定职责，考勤由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Berta Berg把招租纸从门上揭下来，领Mitzi看了后间。屋里只有一张铁床和一只裂了口的洗手盆，窗户对着天井。Mitzi付了六克朗，Berta Berg把钥匙从围裙里解下来，说晚上十点关大门。
  _effects: cash -6 (新城区门牌下的招租纸（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +lodging_secured_in_new_district; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; knowledge “the front door is locked at ten every night”_

- **1949.06** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-union/hired-relative` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  名单上添的是Luise Weiss，一个连公文格式都分不清的远房亲戚。Grete Berg 2把考勤表放在桌上，没有问原因；Mitzi也没有解释，只在签字栏里写得比平时慢。第一个月，Luise Weiss迟到了七次，工资照发。
  _effects: position 档案助理 @mortuary:person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005554; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000124:child; flag +hired_relative; knowledge “考勤表上记了七次迟到，工资一次没有扣。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144_

  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; flag +rival_audited; knowledge “委员会的差旅账目里有两次重复报销。”; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

  Mitzi把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Mitzi忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 20 -> 28; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 15 -> 13; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `kernel-work.task-household-debt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/work-debt-reduced` tags: task, work, debt, household, credit, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi把欠账按日期抄在纸上，先还利息最高的一笔。Katharina Berg翻开米铺账本，把红字划掉一行，写“收到八克朗”。为了凑这笔钱，Mitzi把家里一只旧表送进当铺；Katharina Kral知道后，把自家的赊账往后挪了一个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (偿还米铺欠账（其中8克朗记入债务）); role; flag +debt_reduced; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; knowledge “米铺把赊账利息记在红字栏，先还红字最多的那笔最划算。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1951.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Friedrich Moser借给Mitzi一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Mitzi睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002329:child; mood -0.06000000000000005_

- **1951.08** `absurd15-stage.cast-the-king::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/understudy-contract` tags: performance, theatre, power, irony, work, pretend, vienna, urban
  Rosa Weiss演主角，Mitzi拿到的是替角合同：国王的戏服先按主角的尺寸做，Mitzi只能在排练时穿。导演说这不是坏事，至少不用负责谢幕。首演夜，Mitzi坐在后台，听见前台每一次掌声都像在敲自己的门。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:615; cash 1 (替角的排练补贴); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +understudy_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1952.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Mitzi和Berta Kral合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; household; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1952.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-union/the-files-went-first` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Marie Huber先把Mitzi抽屉里的往来信件搬空，再在会议上说Mitzi从来没有保存过记录。没有人能证明那些信存在过，除了Mitzi自己。散会后，有人让Mitzi回家休息几天，工资照发。Mitzi知道这不是照顾，是让Mitzi别再回来。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; knowledge “我经手的信件在会议前被人搬空了。”; condition; flag +records_removed_

  脚手架搭到第三层，Grete Bauer 2带着一张红纸来了，说有人举报超建。Mitzi说图纸批过，Grete Bauer 2说批的是两层；Grete Lechner 3把图纸翻出来，发现角落里的层数被改过，笔迹不是他的。Grete Bauer 2在脚手架腿上贴了停工令，Anna Hofer在自家窗口看着，窗帘动了一下。

## Josef

Born 1912.01, died 1927.05 (age 15).
Education: apprenticeship 1; occupation: none; roles: infant, child, brickworks_laborer, apprentice.

### State snapshot
- wish-craft [person] (ended 1927.5: owner_died) parameters={"intensity":0.55,"fixation":0.5,"irony":0.3} links={} ticks=1

### 26 subject events

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Josef站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Maria Bauer睡醒哭了一声，Josef把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Josef Bauer回来时，Josef正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000268:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Josef的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Josef在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1919.10** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Josef想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Sigmund Freud听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Josef试着站起来，被Sigmund Freud按回台阶上。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-sigmund-freud_

- **1919.11** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  土豆从围裙里滚出来，Josef蹲下去追，手心一松，三枚十克鲁泽的铜板叮叮当当掉在石板路上。两枚捡回来了，最后一枚滚进排水格栅，在铁条下转了个圈。Josef趴下去够，指甲刮在铁条上。回家Katharina Bauer数完钱，脸色沉下来，Josef站在桌边不敢动。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; mood -0.06_

- **1920.01** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷口有人跑过来，肩膀撞在Josef的胳膊上。烟画卡脱手，被风卷到路中间。Josef追了两步，鞋底在湿石板上打滑，手掌先着地。Lena从后面追上去，在拐角抓住那男孩的袖子，布条撕下来一截，人还是跑了。
  _effects: mood -0.08; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; condition_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  井台边的石沿湿得发亮，Maria Bauer一挣，Josef只能把Maria Bauer抱离地面。院子里晒的床单还没收，Josef背着Maria Bauer在布影里来回走，用一块碎布给Maria Bauer擦鼻涕。等Maria Bauer睡沉，Josef的肩窝已经压出一道红痕，端碗时手指直抖。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000268:child_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Alois Lechner朝Josef招手。Josef挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Josef把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1921.05** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/took-punishment` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Josef把手掌摊在身前，眼睛盯着门后的皮带。几下之后，Katharina Bauer停了手。Josef自己去找扫帚，把地上的碎瓷一片片扫干净，又用湿布擦灶台。两个人在厨房里各做各的，只听见烟纸燃烧的细响。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1921.08** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书在Josef的书包里藏了三天，课间才敢翻两页。还书那天，Josef把书角淋皱的事先说了，又把书页一张张抚平。Anton Weiss翻到最后一页，看了看书脊，说下次别带进院子，书却还是借给了Josef。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work

- **1923.09** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店的门铃响一次，Josef就往柜台边挪一步。Elisabeth Bauer终于从柜台底下抽出一张风景卡，用手掌抹了抹，还是皱的。“拿去吧。”Elisabeth Bauer说完就去招呼别的客人。Josef把卡举到鼻子前闻，一股烟草味，回家路上把多出来的一张卡分给Elisabeth Bauer看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1924.06** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Josef推刨子。Josef推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Josef，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Katharina Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1925.02** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  巷口的风把外套下摆吹起来。旧货贩子闻了闻领子，说给两克朗。Josef把钱卷进袜筒，回家前在楼梯口站了一会儿。Josef Bauer发现衣钩空了，问了三遍，Josef都说没看见，脚趾在鞋里把硬币越踩越紧。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1925.03** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anna Bauer的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Josef把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Anna Bauer看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.03_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/worked-for-dues` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Josef翻遍口袋只有几枚赫勒，便跟管事的说愿替大家抄歌本抵会费。他坐在窗边抄了一下午，手指被墨水染黑，换来的借书权让他捧回一本讲齿轮的旧书，书页里夹着上一个人留下的铅笔字。
  _effects: skill; skill; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +workers_youth_work_trade; mood 0.03_

- **1926.04** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/go-to-brickworks` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “下周一，跟街尾的工头走。”Katharina Bauer说完就把算术本推到桌角。Josef知道再争也没用。Friedrich Hofer 2在门口拦住Josef，把一张夜校地址塞过来，说晚上可以去。Josef把纸条折进鞋垫，第二天就去了砖厂。头一周四克朗，搬砖搬得腰直不起来，纸条在鞋里磨得发毛。
  _effects: role; cash 4 ({subject}砖厂首周工钱); flag +has-evening-school-address; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1926.07** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/enroll-night-class` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Grete Stein在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Josef下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费); education secondary; skill_

  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1927.05** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “太小，手也薄。”Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说完，把Josef推过的木板翻过来，指着上面一道啃痕。Katharina Bauer把布包往肩上提了提，说打扰了。Josef在门口多站了一会儿，直到里面的刨子声把话头盖过去，才跟着Katharina Bauer下楼。

### 326 subject events

- **1917.03** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/home-remedy` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Lena烧得翻来覆去，Josefine Hofer把醋倒进温水，用布蘸着擦手心、脚心，又去灶间煮接骨木汁。勺子送到嘴边，Lena偏头躲，Josefine Hofer就等一会儿再喂。鸡叫时炉火压小，床脚用旧大衣堵住。两天后热度退了，嘴唇起了一层干皮。
  _effects: health 0.03 (土法退烧，热度渐退); mood -0.06_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Hermann Kral朝Lena招手。Lena挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Lena把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Lena站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Grete Weiss在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Lena掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Lena才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Lena的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Lena在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Lena拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Josef Bauer弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Lena接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1921.11** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Lena跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Grete Stein从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Lena前面的大婶一直往后靠，Lena就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Lena的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  Maria Kral家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Lena递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Maria Kral端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Lena把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

  面包房关门前Lena和Josef凑了四赫勒，分到一块凉了的面包。夜里母亲问Lena吃了什么，Lena只答在院子里喝的水。
  _effects: cash -4 (凑钱买面包)_

- **1925.04** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/go-to-brickworks` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “下周一，跟街尾的工头走。”Friedrich Hofer说完就把算术本推到桌角。Lena知道再争也没用。Grete Stein在门口拦住Lena，把一张夜校地址塞过来，说晚上可以去。Lena把纸条折进鞋垫，第二天就去了砖厂。头一周四克朗，搬砖搬得腰直不起来，纸条在鞋里磨得发毛。
  _effects: role; cash 4 ({subject}砖厂首周工钱); flag +has-evening-school-address; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

  _effects: cash -0.1 (只交了一个月会费); relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +workers_youth_quit; mood -0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  Lena数了数前面攒动的人头，把口袋里的硬币又攥紧了些，转身离开队伍。走到街角的教堂时，管风琴正从敞开的门里传出来，Lena在最后一排长凳上坐下，听完整段前奏曲，一个赫勒也没花，只跪得膝盖发酸。出门时与Mitzi Huber道了别，约好下回还来排队。

  在第二个桥洞下，Gustav Winter先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Lena点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子（其中0.04克朗记入债务）); mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Luise Winter；Lena一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Gustav Winter把钟表送进当铺。Lena落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中17.4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:76_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.borrow-handcart/only-the-cart` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  开口时只提了手推车，钱的事咽回去。对方爽快地把车钥匙样的铁钩递过来，说用完了搁回院里就行。十克朗改去当铺想办法，一件旧外套和一只怀表抵了十二克朗，赎期三个月，票据折好收在证件夹里。
  _effects: cash 12 (当铺抵当旧外套和怀表换来的钱); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000245:child; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.09** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.pay-later` tags: home, market, food, social
  Lena捏着三个克朗硬币站在玛丽亚的菜摊前，土豆和洋葱已经装进布袋。周五结清时，玛丽亚用粉笔在小黑板上划掉名字，Maria Kral点点头。
  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

  Lena难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Maria Winter；Lena一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Gustav Winter把钟表送进当铺。Lena落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。

  Elisabeth Berg把废料推到他面前，说做完我看。他握着凿子时手腕发僵，第一个榫肩削多了，第二个缝里能透光。师傅用指甲划过合缝处，没说话，把料子留下。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1934.02** `bc18-crime.failed-burglary::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/caught-in-darkroom` tags: crime, burglary, mistake, absurd, police, legal, comic, vienna
  显影液被Emil Stein碰翻，整盆药水泼在一张婚礼合影上。新郎的脸变成一团灰雾，店主抓住Lena的领子；Katharina Bauer来后，两人被关了一夜。第二天，Lena交了罚款三克朗，Emil Stein被新娘的家人追着要赔照片；那顶帽子也留在了拘留室。
  _effects: cash -3 (破坏暗房的罚款（其中3克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.04; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:112; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; condition; flag +destroyed_a_wedding_portrait; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1934.02** `r3-crime.domestic-theft::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/parent-reported-it` tags: crime, family, poverty, shame, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  Friedrich Hofer在派出所门口站了很久，最后还是推门进去了。Lena被叫去问话时，Friedrich Hofer坐在长椅另一头，手杖横在膝盖上。警察问“确定要告吗”，Friedrich Hofer说“不告她还会偷”，声音很轻，Lena却听得清清楚楚。
  _effects: cash -3 (米缸后面藏着一只银勺（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +family_reported_theft_to_police; role; relation rival with person-friedrich-hofer; relation_end kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1934.09** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Lena对Luise Weiss说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Luise Weiss把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Lena的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000124:child_

  把那张字据推回去，说房租和母亲的药费都在桌上摆着，这名字不能签。前两次的借条从证件夹里翻出来放在一起，摊平让对方看清。对方把纸卷起来收进外套，门带得比平常重，此后在街上碰见只当没认出来。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 31; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.03** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-partner-bought-it-secretly` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  Gustav Winter在开奖前偷偷去了烟草铺，用买菜剩下的钱买了那组号码，把票藏在糖罐后面。号码中了，Gustav Winter把票放在餐桌上，说本来想等Lena生日再讲。Lena先是不信，随后笑得把椅子往后推了一截；两个人坐在厨房里数奖金，数到一半又停下来烧水。
  _effects: cash 70 (the lottery prize bought with the grocery money); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:148; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; reputation 0.029999999999999995; flag +partner_bought_the_ticket; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.02; mood 0.02999999999999997_

  队伍在公路边停下，Otto Weiss在田埂上支了一口锅，一勺开水两分钱。Lena替Maria Kral买了一勺，Otto Weiss又多舀了半勺，说“老人不算钱”。Alois Hofer在桥头喊队伍继续走，Maria Kral把搪瓷杯揣进怀里，水洒了一路。
  _effects: cash -2 (公路边的开水锅); food; flag +bought_hot_water_on_the_road; relation friend with person-maria-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1935.08** `r13-turn.recovery::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/healed-cleanly` tags: health, recovery, medicine, body, hope, vienna, urban, crisis
  Gustav Winter 2把最后一层绷带剪开，伤口已经收成一条浅红色的线。Lena试着弯了弯手指，关节发出一点响声，但不疼了。Luise Weiss在门口说，能自己拿勺子吃饭就是好了。Lena当天下午走回院子，第一次没有扶墙。
  _effects: health 0.15 (拆掉绷带的那天); flag +recovered_from_wound; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000124:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  Lena在雪地里捡到一只缝线开裂的钱包，里面有三克朗、一张彩票和一张写着地址的纸。Lena按地址送回去，开门的老太太说钱包不是她的，却把彩票塞给Lena，说“你拿着吧，我这辈子没中过”。
  _effects: state_add af17-lucky-idiot state:world-vienna-1912:189; cash 3 (the wallet’s owner refused the money); flag +returned_the_snow_wallet; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +lucky_idiot; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: mood 0.08000000000000002; condition; flag +return_visa_granted; relation friend with person-ferdinand-winter; knowledge “returning exiles must report to the police”_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-slips-away` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Lena把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 0 -> 0; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena把Rosa Winter的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Rosa Winter今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena把Anna Winter的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Anna Winter今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/the-bill-comes-due` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  好运气没有出现。Lena错过电车，鞋跟断在环城大道的石缝里，回家才发现钥匙也丢了。邻居没有安慰，只说“总算轮到你了”，语气像在结一笔旧账。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 12 -> 11; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 20 -> 30; cash -1 (修鞋和配钥匙); mood -0.029999999999999985_

- **1937.01** `p10-migration-tasks.ask-travel-money/uncle-part-loan-note` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, loan, relative
  舅父从裤袋里数出十五克朗推过来，说剩下的让他去车站帮车夫装货抵工钱。舅父在灯下写了一张欠条，写明三个月内还清，他签了名，把钱对折放进内袋，欠条留给舅父收在账簿里。
  _effects: cash 15 (舅父借给的路费); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; flag +owes_uncle_15_kronen; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1937.07** `hs18-queue.figure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/give-your-place` tags: historical_figure, queue, recognition, bread, witness, history, vienna, urban
  队伍往前挪时，Lena认出前面那个把帽檐压低的人——报纸上见过那张脸，Sigmund Freud。对方手里也攥着一张配给证，指节冻得发白。Lena把自己的位置让出来，说自己去另一条队；Sigmund Freud点了一下头，没有报名字。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:222; relation acquaintance with person-sigmund-freud; food; flag +yielded_place_to_historical_figure_

- **1937.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Ferdinand Weiss 2说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Ferdinand Weiss 2陪着Lena去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; mood -0.1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1938.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Katharina Winter这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 31; mood 0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005915_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Lena在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Ferdinand Weiss 2记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Lena带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1938.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Gustav Winter在楼梯间坐了很久；Lena在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Lena的名字；此后邻居提起Lena，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

- **1938.01** `bc18-luck.civic-mascot::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/luck-blamed-for-fire` tags: luck, charity, public, reputation, absurd, social, suspicion, vienna
  仓库失火那晚，委员会的活动棚因为风向改变而保住了。第二天，Hermann Winter却在门口拦住Lena，说有人看见Lena在火起前从仓库边走过；幸运不能证明清白，反而像一种从别人那里借来的东西。Lena把铜别针还给她，回家路上第一次觉得，好运也会留下脚印。
  _effects: cash -1 (被委员会辞退后的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.04; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 30 -> 45; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; condition; flag +luck_blamed_for_fire; mood -0.04000000000000001_

  Lena忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Lena听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 80 -> 72; mood -0.020000000000000004_

  Lena把Luise Winter的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Luise Winter今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 39 -> 47; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 39 -> 36; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1938.08** `r2-faith.tithe-in-kind::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/traded-labor-for-tithe` tags: faith, tithe, farm, harvest, conflict, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena跟Karl Kraus商量，麦子只交半袋，剩下的用修教堂屋顶的工抵。Karl Kraus让Lena先上去换三片瓦，Lena在钟楼边上干了两天，下来时腿还在抖。Anna Bauer在下面递瓦，说这比交麦子划算。
  _effects: cash -1 (第十袋黑麦（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +paid_tithe_with_roof_repairs; skill; relation colleague with person-karl-kraus; knowledge “the church roof needs three new tiles every winter”_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Berta Winter。接生的人收六克朗，Gustav Winter在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Lena起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:276_

- **1939.01** `hs18-occupation.piano::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refuse-to-sign` tags: occupation, piano, requisition, witness, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  Lena把笔推回去，说钢琴的主人就住在隔壁。Ferdinand Weiss 2没有争，只让工人把琴盖也搬走，又记下Lena的门牌号。卡车开走时，Ferdinand Lechner家的窗子开了又关；Lena站在空出来的门洞前，听见自己的呼吸声。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:277; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; reputation 0.02; flag +refused_piano_receipt; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/the-bill-comes-due` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  好运气没有出现。Lena错过电车，鞋跟断在环城大道的石缝里，回家才发现钥匙也丢了。邻居没有安慰，只说“总算轮到你了”，语气像在结一笔旧账。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 10 -> 9; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 55 -> 65; cash -1 (修鞋和配钥匙（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.03_

- **1939.01** `r13-fam.ask-relative-money::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/loan-with-strings` tags: task, family, debt, money, relative, request, vienna, urban
  Hedwig把钱带来，却要Lena在借条上写明：明年开春若还不上，就替Hedwig家的孩子交学费。Lena盯着那行字看了很久，还是按了手印。Hedwig收起借条，说亲戚也要有凭据；Lena把钱揣进怀里，觉得比借来的还沉。
  _effects: cash 10 (信里只写了一个数目); condition; flag +accepted_conditional_family_loan; role; relation kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the loan is secured by a promise to pay school fees”_

- **1939.02** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Lena在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1939.08** `r2-law.citizenship-papers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/oath-administered` tags: law, citizenship, papers, exam, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi Lechner 2问Lena国歌第二句是什么，又让她说出本区议员的名字。Lena答对了一半，Mitzi Lechner 2在表格上写“基本合格”，让下周来宣誓。Gustav Winter 2在门外等着，手里拿着一面小旗；Lena出来时，Mitzi Lechner 2又在后面补了一句：别迟到。
  _effects: cash -8 (入籍宣誓前的口试（其中8克朗记入债务）); flag +citizenship_oath_scheduled; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; knowledge “the oath ceremony is held every second Tuesday”_

- **1940.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Anton Winter。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Gustav Winter把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Lena躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:295_

- **1940.01** `af17-luck.quiet-windfall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/kept-the-nine-kronen` tags: luck, windfall, daily, quiet, money, social, coincidence, vienna
  Lena把钱包塞进大衣内袋，用那九克朗买了两袋煤、一双不漏水的靴子和半磅咖啡。第二天，歌剧院门口贴出一张手写启事，说一位老妇人在找一只鳄鱼皮钱包，里面是她丈夫留下的最后一张站票。Lena路过时把围巾往上拉了拉，靴子很暖，走路却没有声音。
  _effects: cash 9 (留下的钱包里的钱); state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 65 -> 80; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1940.01** `state_reaction:af17-lucky-idiot/windfall-talk` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  咖啡馆里有人问Lena，是不是替哪个议员跑过腿，或者认识一个会看牌的女人。Lena说自己只是运气好；这句话说出口，连Lena自己都觉得像在遮掩。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 80 -> 92; reputation -0.02; flag +luck_explained_as_a_deal; mood -0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007067_

- **1940.01** `state_transition:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-becomes-a-scandal` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  Lena从「走运的傻瓜」转入另一段处境：怀疑积累到足以把好运变成一桩公开丑闻
  _effects: state_transition af17-lucky-idiot -> public-scandal; reputation -0.04; flag +luck_became_a_scandal; flag -lucky_idiot_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Lena又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 90 -> 92_

- **1940.01** `r13-civ.quarantine::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::r13-civ.quarantine-crisis/crisis-lodger-fever` tags: health, quarantine, epidemic, neighbor, confinement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi半夜敲墙，说同屋的人烧得说胡话。Lena隔着板壁问要不要去叫医生，Mitzi说医生来了也只给退热粉，还要收出诊费。天亮时，楼梯间多了一股石灰水味，Grete Huber把白线画到了隔壁门口。
  _effects: cash -2 (门框上的石灰线); flag +crisis_quarantine_house; knowledge “a second household on the stair was placed under quarantine”_

- **1940.04** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Lena在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Lena去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001336:child_

  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Anton Winter。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Gustav Winter把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Lena躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:324_

- **1941.01** `pl19-bridge.public-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-room-with-a-list` tags: scandal, protection, network, press, media, rumor, bridge, vienna
  一位官员在散场后拦住Lena，说可以借一间会议室，每周二晚上用，条件是名单由他过目。Lena看见他手里还攥着当天的报纸，头版折得很整齐；那间会议室后来真的开了灯，只是门口多了一把没人坐的椅子。
  _effects: state_add pl19-protected-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:325; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; flag +official_room; knowledge “会议室每周二晚上开灯，名单要先给一位官员过目。”; flag +protected_scandal_active_

- **1941.01** `r1-civ.requisition-cart::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/room-requisitioned` tags: war, requisition, property, army, shortage, vienna, urban, crisis
  Ferdinand Weiss 2要征用Lena家朝街的一间屋做办公室，给一张“使用许可”。Lena说屋里住着老人，Ferdinand Weiss 2说可以留一间后屋。搬东西那天，Helene Kral来帮忙，把墙上的十字架摘下来，说“这个先收好”。
  _effects: cash -3 (征收单上的马（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood -0.1; condition; flag +room_requisitioned; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; knowledge “the use permit leaves one back room to the household”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Lena忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Lena听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 92 -> 84; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Lena被叫到学校，老师说Anton Winter这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Lena没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 18; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1941.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Berta Winter这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 31; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007916_

- **1941.12** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/collect-the-benefit` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  邻居出主意说，死者家属可以领一笔丧葬补助。Lena让Elisabeth Stein冒充远房表姐，自己在旁边按了手印；钱到手后，两人在厨房里分成三份，第三份留着买煤。第二个星期，Josef Winter寄来一封信，说抚恤金已经核准，请家属来拍一张遗像；Lena把信压在盐罐下面，盐受潮结了块。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒领的丧葬补助分成); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:326; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; condition; flag +collected_own_funeral_benefit; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Anna Winter。接生的人收六克朗，Gustav Winter在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Lena起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:345_

- **1942.01** `pl19-scandal.alibi::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-alibi-has-a-price` tags: marriage, alibi, scandal, protection, family, betrayal, vienna, urban
  Gustav Winter愿意作证，但要在分居协议上多写一条：房子归Gustav Winter，Lena保留探视权。Lena签了字，Gustav Winter在调查员面前把时间说得很清楚，连钟点都编好了。Lena忽然明白，这不是帮忙，是一次结账。
  _effects: cash -3 (the alibi was paid for in a separation agreement（其中3克朗记入债务）); relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 65 -> 73; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.leverage 35 -> 45; flag +alibi_price; knowledge “分居协议上多写了一条：房子归配偶。”_

  有人在一场公开活动里问起Lena的“私人安排”，语气客气，问题却像一根针。Lena笑着答了别的事，回家后才发现手心全是汗。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.publicHeat 40 -> 52; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 73 -> 67; reputation -0.029999999999999995_

  有人替Lena挡了一次采访，随后在走廊里提起自己儿子的学徒期满。Lena知道这不是闲聊；当天晚上，那张写着名字的纸条被压在糖罐下面，像一枚没有付清的硬币。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.leverage 45 -> 57; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 67 -> 63; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 45 -> 50; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Lena学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Lena的材料齐全。年底，Lena从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 39; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 47; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 50 -> 60; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.leverage 57 -> 65; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.publicHeat 52 -> 56; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.11** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.dispute-rate` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铜板一枚枚落在桌面上，比说好的少了十五赫勒。Lena没去捡，先问Otto Berg下捆还送不送。Otto Berg含糊应了一声，拎起布包就走。Franz Lechner这才进来，说下回得先讲好价。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1944.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Berta Winter。接生的人收六克朗，Gustav Winter在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Lena起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:400_

- **1944.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/small-surplus` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  账目连续第二年没有超支，Josef Winter在报告里加了一句“未发现异常”。Lena用结余给夜班守卫换了炉子，又把旧账本按年份装进箱子；没有人写报道，但来办事的人发现窗口开得比从前早。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.06; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; flag +quiet_surplus; knowledge “审计报告里只有一句“未发现异常”，但窗口提前开门被办事的人记住了。”_

- **1944.07** `r3-crime.police-raid::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::political-week/hid-the-neighbors-son` tags: crime, police, tenement, fear, politics, vienna, urban, crisis
  Katharina Hofer的儿子躲在煤窖里，Lena把一袋土豆盖在他身上。Ferdinand Weiss 2用手电扫过窖口，问下面有没有人，Lena说只有老鼠。警察走后，Katharina Hofer抓着Lena的手一直道谢；一个月后，局里却以“妨碍搜查”传唤了Lena。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +hid_neighbor_from_police_raid; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000990:child; knowledge “the neighbor’s son was hiding in the coal cellar”_

  十二个人挤在印刷所的排字间，Lena把一张包糖纸铺在字盘上，写下第一条：工伤每周三克朗，丧葬十五克朗。Katharina Hofer问钱不够怎么办，Lena说先收四克朗入会费，账本轮流保管。Alois Hofer后来在注册处盖章，说名字不能带“工会”。
  _effects: cash -4 (互助会的第一本账（其中4克朗记入债务）); flag +founded_mutual_aid_society; role; organization_membership; organization_funds; knowledge “the society cannot use the word union in its registered name”_

  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.45 -> 0.6; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/the-logbook-mislaid` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Lena半个月的夜班钱；Lena没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1947.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.borrow-handcart/handcart-and-ten` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  星期天搬家的前一晚，有人坐在印厂友人家的厨房里把事说全：车要借，十克朗也要借。对方合上账本，从抽屉里数出两张五克朗，说月底先还三克朗，剩下的按月摊。手推车的轮轴有点响，搬之前得先上油。
  _effects: cash 10 (友人借给搬家应急的十克朗); relation friend with person-marie-huber; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Katharina Winter这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 13 -> 16; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011331_

- **1947.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Alois Moser在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Alois Moser敲开Lena的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1947.08** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-sell-furniture-move` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  邻居用麻绳把锅和铺盖捆在一起，打了个死结。他说城南有更便宜的屋子，但离厂远。你蹲在地上把最后几件衣服塞进布袋，听见看门人在楼下把垃圾桶挪来挪去。
  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_
