- **1947.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  下午的活是搬纱锭，Lena蹲下去再站起来眼前发黑，扶墙站了一会儿。工友老约瑟夫把自己的水杯推过来，说去诊所开个假条吧，她摇头，说假条要扣钱。
  _effects: condition; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/private-sublet` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, household
  Lena从报纸小广告上找到一间转租屋，付了三十先令“钥匙钱”。墙皮一碰就掉，冬天窗框漏风；Mitzi说这一带都这样，先住下再说。
  _effects: residence; cash -30 (key money and rent in advance（其中30克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1948.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth
  Lena难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Katharina Winter；Lena一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Gustav Winter把钟表送进当铺。Lena落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）)_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena被叫到学校，老师说Berta Winter这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Lena没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 25 -> 20; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 57 -> 62; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.02** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-got-scoop` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  风从教堂的拱廊里灌过来，队伍里没人说话，只听见罐子碰罐子的声音。Helene Weiss 2排在前面，领完没走，站在墙根等Lena，把自己的面包掰了一半递过去。
  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1948.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena把Maria Bauer和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Lena把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1949.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Gustav Winter在楼梯间坐了很久；Lena在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Lena的名字；此后邻居提起Lena，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1949.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-budget-was-already-spent` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  账本上那笔“市政美化”已经花掉了：前年在广场立了一座铜像，去年给铜像换了底座。Lena仍把许可证签了，因为申请人已经把材料款付给了铜像的承包商。冬天，铜像底下多了一圈没有报批的围栏。
  _effects: reputation -0.04000000000000001; flag +budget_already_spent; knowledge “市政美化预算已经被铜像和底座用掉，许可证的款项来自同一个承包商。”; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144_

- **1949.01** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/give-place-to-mother` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Katharina Winter怀里的孩子脸烧得通红，Lena往后退了半步，把用粉笔写的号码塞进Katharina Winter手里。药房伙计收了六十赫勒，给一瓶褐色药水；回家路上Lena一阵阵发冷。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:008681:child_

  Luise Weiss在办事处说了Lena一整个下午的坏话，从欠债说到那场没人看见的火灾。旁边的人没有替Lena说一句话。几天后，Lena发现自己的名字从互助会的名单上被划掉了，Luise Weiss只说“我也要养家”。
  _effects: reputation -0.08000000000000002; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

- **1950.01** `r14-soc.confront-rumor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/stopped-the-story` tags: task, rumor, reputation, neighbor_conflict, confrontation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena在洗衣房等到Katharina Berg来打水，把门从里面插上，说今天把话说清楚。Katharina Berg起初还笑，后来声音低下去，承认是听来的。第二天，院子里的话少了一半。Lena照常晾床单，只是不再借给Katharina Berg洗衣盆。那天晚上，Katharina Berg家的水桶在洗衣房里放了一夜，没有人去提。第二天清早，Katharina Berg把水桶提走了。
  _effects: mood 0.09999999999999998; reputation 0.08000000000000002; flag +stopped_rumor; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1950.03** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena和Helene Weiss 2谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Lena家，钥匙只在白天给。Helene Weiss 2说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Alois Winter 2听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Lena把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1950.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  护士把冰袋按在Lena额上，水顺着鬓角流进衣领。Berta Berg坐在床脚，数着窗外渐渐亮起的铺面门板，说回去给炉子添块煤。隔壁床的老头整夜在喊一个女人的名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧)_

  编制委员会只用了十分钟就否掉了Friedrich Moser的名字：预算冻结，岗位归并，连桌子都要搬去仓库。Lena把那张表折起来，答应明年再试。Friedrich Moser说没关系，第二天去了另一家事务所面试。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002329:child; flag +protege_hiring_refused; knowledge “编制委员会今年冻住了所有新增岗位。”_

- **1951.03** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Lena端着一只旧锅去敲Rosa Weiss的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Rosa Weiss让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Lena连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003546:child; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.06** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/blocked-the-permit` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Lena把一份扩建许可证压在抽屉最下面，理由是排水和消防通道不合规。申请人Helene Kral在走廊里等到下班，最后只拿到一张写着“补件”的纸条。Lena知道这份许可明年还会再来，但今年不会从自己的桌上出去。
  _effects: reputation 0.01999999999999999; skill; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; flag +blocked_permit; knowledge “扩建申请的排水图与消防通道不符合现行规定。”_

- **1951.09** `bc18-fame.wrong-bust::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/plaque-stays` tags: fame, mistaken_identity, museum, public, invention, absurd, status, humiliation
  市立蜡像馆新添了一间“发明家厅”。Grete Bauer 2把Lena请到绿绒椅子上，说照片是从旧报纸上剪的，名字也核对过三遍。揭幕那天，Lena看见自己的蜡像握着一根安全火柴，铭牌上写着一个从未属于Lena的专利号；掌声很整齐，Grete Bauer 2把下一场讲解的酬劳塞进口袋，动作像在递一块糖。
  _effects: cash 4 (蜡像馆的讲解酬劳); state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:614; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; flag +wax_museum_inventor; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Lena被叫到学校，老师说Berta Winter这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Lena没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。

- **1952.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-audience-gets-bored` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  观众开始厌倦同一个故事。园艺会请了更年轻的发明家，女子学校把Lena的名字从海报上撤下；Lena在咖啡馆听见有人说“那个人还在讲火柴”。同一个月，一张报纸把旧照片配上新标题，问Lena是否已经“过气”。纠正的冲动又回来，但没有人再提问。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 12 -> 4; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 8 -> 20; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 0; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `p10-migration-tasks.move-district/move-three-loads-settled` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, moving, district
  第二趟推到桥心，邻居扶了一把歪掉的柜子。孩子抱着书包跟在车后，问学校远不远。他把钥匙塞进衣兜，听见新房东在楼梯口说：季度租，少一个赫勒也不行。
  _effects: cash -9 (结清旧屋煤账、给新房东交部分季度租金与手推车租用费（其中9克朗记入债务）); residence; mood -0.050000000000000044; knowledge “新房子的房东只按季度收租，一次要交十克朗以上”_

- **1952.02** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-refused` tags: faith, social, family, home
  登记员翻遍册子，说这户上个月已经领过。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; reputation -0.039999999999999994; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1952.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Alois Moser说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Alois Moser陪着Lena去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1952.07** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-swap-tips` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  台阶上蹲着几个等消息的人，Gustav Winter把烟盒纸摊在膝盖上画路线，说北站货栈的管事认字就行，不认登记所那一套。Lena把那半截铅笔头借来，在纸角写上时辰，折好收进内袋。
  _effects: flag +has_work_lead; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

  _effects: mood 0.12; cash -1 (寄往鲁尔的信和明信片邮费); knowledge “哥哥在鲁尔矿区做工，还活着”_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Lena把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Lena来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1954.01** `r13-civ.join-charity::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/accepted-as-visitor` tags: social, charity, membership, poverty, respectability, vienna, urban, postwar
  Grete Bauer 2把登记簿摊在门房的小桌上，让Lena写下姓名和住址。Lena写完后，Grete Bauer 2在名字旁边画了一个小十字，说下周去给孤老送煤。本子上前面几行，墨迹已经褪成了褐色。
  _effects: cash -1 (慈善会登记簿上的空行); reputation 0.04000000000000001; flag +joined_charity_visiting_round; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

- **1954.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Lena听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Josef Huber扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.03** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household, injury
  Lena把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Lena的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart（其中8克朗记入债务）); health -0.020000000000000004 (moving in winter)_

- **1954.06** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena把通知念给Emil Stein听，Emil Stein摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Lena去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.06** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Helene Weiss 3排在前面，回头替Lena看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Lena用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Lena没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_

  第二轮投票是十七比十七。按章程，主席可以投第二票；Lena把自己的名字念了两遍，会议室里没有人笑。散会后，Ferdinand Weiss 2在门口说下一次会带更多的人来，Lena说椅子够用。
  _effects: reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.019999999999999907; organization_funds; flag +tie_broken_by_chair; knowledge “章程允许主席在平票时投第二票，但没有人记得上一次使用是什么时候。”_

- **1955.01** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Lena走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1956.04** `r2-law.eviction-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/eviction-ordered` tags: law, eviction, housing, court, tenement, vienna, urban, postwar
  Ferdinand Weiss 2说欠租事实清楚，合同也到期了，当庭判令腾房，限十五天。Helene Lechner的律师问能不能立即执行，Ferdinand Weiss 2说十五天已经是最短。Lena问孩子上学怎么办，Ferdinand Weiss 2说这个庭管不了学校；Grete Bauer 2在门口等着，把自家的板车推了过来。
  _effects: cash -6 (驱逐听证的十五分钟（其中6克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +eviction_ordered_by_court; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; knowledge “the court gives fifteen days and does not rule on schooling”_

- **1956.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Ferdinand Bauer 3拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

- **1956.09** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; reputation -0.03; mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

- **1957.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Ferdinand Bauer 3嫌Lena的床单滴水滴到Ferdinand Bauer 3家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Lena下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Lena把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Ferdinand Bauer 3那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

- **1957.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/clean-audit` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Katharina Berg带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Lena把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Katharina Berg抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; organization_funds; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +passed_audit; knowledge “审计抽查了十二份合同，每一份都能对上签字人。”_

- **1957.01** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sibling-medicine-bill/split-per-head` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Lena把旧练习本翻到空白一页，写下母亲、自己和Hedwig的名字。十四克朗的药费单，Lena和Hedwig各摊五克朗，余下四克朗从母亲的养老金里扣；Lena把五克朗硬币一枚枚摞在桌沿，Hedwig盯着那一栏看了半天，也从围裙口袋里摸出钱来。
  _effects: cash -5 (分摊母亲药费中自己的一份（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912_

- **1957.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Lena在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Lena拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Otto Stein找到Lena时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002917:child_

  Lena对Luise 2说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Luise 2把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Lena的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002172:child_

## Anton

Born 1912.01, died 1919.01 (age 7).
Education: none 0; occupation: none; roles: infant, child.

### State snapshot
No mechanism state was ever attached.

### 3 subject events

- **1914.03** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/a-word-for-bread` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Anton指着桌上的面包说出一个短词，声音轻得像在试音。Josefine Hofer把面包掰下一角，泡进热水里喂给孩子；Anton吃完又指了一次，这次说得更清楚。厨房里的人都笑了，连灶上的水汽也像在往上冒。
  _effects: food; mood 0.03; flag +first_word_bread_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Alois Lechner家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Anton递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Alois Lechner端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Anton把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; skill_

- **1919.01** `statistical_death/violence` tags: death
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang卷入一场冲突，头部和胸口受了重伤。旁人把人抬回家，夜里伤势恶化。Hedwig在场。
  _effects: death (暴力)_

- **1917.05** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  围巾从Hedwig的头一直裹到脚，Friedrich Hofer抱着人下楼，在电车踏板上差点滑倒。急诊窗口先收押金，Friedrich Hofer把内袋里的票子一张张抹平，数出六克朗。Hedwig躺在观察室的窄床上，头顶的灯管嗡嗡响，Friedrich Hofer的手一直按着被角。

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Hedwig的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Hedwig在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.030000000000000006; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Hermann Kral把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Hedwig伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Hermann Kral从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Hedwig手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Hedwig一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral_

- **1920.06** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书在Hedwig的书包里藏了三天，课间才敢翻两页。还书那天，Hedwig把书角淋皱的事先说了，又把书页一张张抚平。Grete Stein翻到最后一页，看了看书脊，说下次别带进院子，书却还是借给了Hedwig。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Maria Berg朝Hedwig招手。Hedwig挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Hedwig把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; skill_

- **1921.10** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Hedwig想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Friedrich Hofer听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Hedwig试着站起来，被Friedrich Hofer按回台阶上。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Ferdinand Winter在绳子那头喊。Hedwig踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Ferdinand Winter数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Hedwig把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Mitzi Huber把Hedwig拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Hedwig把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Hedwig蹲下把两包并排，按Mitzi Huber说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1924.09** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-follow-without-paying` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Hedwig口袋里没有十赫勒，却还是跟着队伍走到车站。修女在栅栏边拦住Hedwig，翻了翻名册，说没交钱的孩子不能上车；Mitzi Lechner在车厢门口回头看。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.030000000000000013_

- **1925.07** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/go-to-brickworks` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “下周一，跟街尾的工头走。”Friedrich Hofer说完就把算术本推到桌角。Hedwig知道再争也没用。Grete Stein在门口拦住Hedwig，把一张夜校地址塞过来，说晚上可以去。Hedwig把纸条折进鞋垫，第二天就去了砖厂。头一周四克朗，搬砖搬得腰直不起来，纸条在鞋里磨得发毛。
  _effects: role; cash 4 ({subject}砖厂首周工钱); flag +has-evening-school-address; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/went-once-and-left` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Hedwig交了第一个月的会费，进去坐了一刻钟。屋里太吵，下棋的争论盖过了念书的声音，他借的书也被人翻得卷了边。下个月他路过门口，没再进去交十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (只交了一个月会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; flag +workers_youth_quit; mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1926.07** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/enroll-night-class` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anton Weiss在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Hedwig下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费); education secondary; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/printshop-apprentice` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Hedwig下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance); role_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.trade-school-application/switched-to-church-class` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  他从职业学校门口走开时，听见里面在搬长凳。拐过街角，教堂侧门开着，Friedrich Hofer 2正往黑板上写乘法表，招手让他进来坐最后一排。
  _effects: mood 0.03; skill; flag +church_tutoring; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/hand-caught` tags: adolescence, labour, union
  Hedwig在压纸时右手被滚轴卷进去，工头拉下电闸，血滴在铅字盘上。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让人把Hedwig送到医院，医药费先由厂里垫付。
  _effects: condition; cash -4 (hospital transport)_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.learn-the-task/polished-type-redo` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Hedwig以为字越亮越好，拿干布把每个铅字都擦了一遍。开机后油墨从字面滑开，第一张广告上的价钱像被水泡过。Friedrich Lechner没骂，只把纸推到Hedwig面前，让Hedwig自己看。倒字、重排、再上机，等到最后一张印清，窗外已经点灯，Hedwig的腿站得发木。
  _effects: health -0.04 (饿着肚子加班重排); mood -0.06; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1928.07** `vienna-youth-threshold.first-wage/half-for-shoes` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  鞋铺橱窗里一双结实的工作鞋标价四克朗二十赫勒，Hedwig的鞋底已经能吸进雪水。回家后Hedwig把信封拆开，一半放在桌上，一半揣进内袋，说下个月不向家里要鞋钱。Friedrich Hofer没说话，只把桌上的钱一张张抹平。
  _effects: cash 4 (工资的一半交给家里); cash -4.2 (买一双不漏水的工作鞋); health 0.05 (脚不再整日泡在湿冷里); relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/joined-and-borrowed` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Hedwig跟着Elisabeth Bauer走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1930.01** `task-vienna-bourgeois-youth.family-business-or-study/ledger-and-evening-class` tags: bourgeois, education, culture, salon, university, family, task, task:education
  Hedwig接过账房钥匙，同时报了夜间的经济课程，一周三晚。白天对账，晚上听课，到周末眼睛发涩。Friedrich Hofer没反对，只是把钥匙串上的铜牌擦了一遍交给他。
  _effects: cash 8 (管账的月钱); skill; mood -0.04000000000000001; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1930.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.work-injury/rag-and-work` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Gustav Winter 2趁工头走开，塞过来一小截胶布，说别让油泥进伤口。Hedwig嗯了一声，换班铃响时指头上的布已经黑了一半，指甲盖下透着紫。
  _effects: condition; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1930.08** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.dispute-rate` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铜板一枚枚落在桌面上，比说好的少了十五赫勒。Hedwig没去捡，先问Anna Kral下捆还送不送。Anna Kral含糊应了一声，拎起布包就走。Luise Lechner这才进来，说下回得先讲好价。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1930.08** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Hedwig把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Gustav Winter 2放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Gustav Berg在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1930.09** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Mitzi在午休时把本子摊在Hedwig的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Hedwig从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Mitzi当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.04_

- **1931.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.read-in-aisle` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Hedwig站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Gustav Winter 2在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Hedwig摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1931.09** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.opened-letter/opened-letter-cold-reply` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Hedwig信了听来的话，把回信写得又短又冷，只问对方家里到底嫌哪一样，写完封了口，第二天一早塞进街角的邮筒。此后走廊里碰见Ferdinand Winter，两边都只点头，话绕开信的事走；Hedwig与Luise Weiss之间那点热乎气，也被这四行字压凉了。
  _effects: mood -0.060000000000000026; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000124:child_

- **1931.09** `vienna-adult-social.wage-loan/wage-loan.granted` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Hedwig在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的账房里向Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne开口预支十二克朗，因为家里的煤和面包钱同时到期。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne从抽屉里数出钞票，让会计在发薪日扣回，并记下“下不为例”。
  _effects: cash 12 (预支工资，先拿到现金); reputation 0.049999999999999996_

- **1931.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Gustav Winter 2一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Gustav Winter 2的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1932.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Gustav Winter 2嫌Hedwig的床单滴水滴到Gustav Winter 2家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Hedwig下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Hedwig把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Gustav Winter 2那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003408_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Hedwig不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Hedwig拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Hedwig的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

- **1932.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.no-baptism` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  “洗礼证明。”Gustav Weiss把手一摊。Hedwig只有出生纸，说教区在城另一头。站务员看了眼钟，说名册按时候收。他跑出去搭车，回来时柜门已经上锁。
  _effects: cash -1 (往返教区的车钱); mood -0.07; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1932.02** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  汤锅冒着热气，Hedwig盛出两勺倒进Elisabeth Bauer带来的罐子里。Elisabeth Bauer说了声谢谢就匆匆上楼。隔天罐子还回来，锅底搁着一小块猪油。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1932.09** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Hedwig跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Marie Huber探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber_

- **1932.12** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Hedwig请楼下的Mitzi Bauer白天来照看Friedrich Hofer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Mitzi Bauer上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Hedwig下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Friedrich Hofer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; health 0.04 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Hedwig和Luise Lechner合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; household; mood 0.06_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.listen-only` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Hedwig坐在后厅角落的啤酒箱上听完整场排练，Katharina Berg中途走过来问要不要在名册上签字，Hedwig说再想想。散场时人们三三两两去前厅续杯，Hedwig把空杯子放回柜台，顺着酒馆后门的巷子走回家，嘴里还留着半段旋律；这一晚没花钱，也和Katharina Berg多说了两句话。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1933.07** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Hedwig对Anna Kral说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Anna Kral把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Hedwig的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1933.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Katharina Berg把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Hedwig去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状)_

- **1934.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-accusation-fails` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Berta Berg在众人面前说Hedwig用诅咒害人。没有人接话，因为上个月Berta Berg也用同样的话说过另一个人。事情没有闹大，但Hedwig发现，从此每次走进那间屋子，都有人先看Hedwig的手。

- **1934.01** `state_reaction:suspected-witchcraft/another-name` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Berta Berg在指控Hedwig的人面前添了一句话，说Hedwig曾经在夜里念过谁的名字。那句话没有证据，但登记册上又多了一行。Hedwig后来才知道，添这句话的人就是Berta Berg。

  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 0 -> 1; mood -0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004225_

- **1934.01** `r13-work.interview-prep::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/borrowed-collar` tags: work, interview, shop, preparation, class, vienna, urban, crisis
  Franz Moser把自己结婚时的硬领借给Hedwig，又用湿布把领口擦了一遍。镜子只有巴掌大，Hedwig练了三遍怎么进门、怎么把帽子拿在手里。Emil Huber的柜台在两条街外，招的是会写字的人；Hedwig把地址抄在纸片上，睡前又看了一遍。
  _effects: cash -1 (借来的硬领); flag +work13_prepared_for_interview; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1934.03** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Maria Kral把会费账本推回去，说外头那个组织答应给煤，只要名单。Hedwig把账本合上，用一块旧布包好塞进缝纫机底下，说先看看谁在往外递话。两人都没再提这事。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_district_list_private_

- **1934.03** `statistical_infection`
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1934.04** `statistical_recovery`
  Hedwig从肺结核中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1934.10** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Helene Weiss 2却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Hedwig把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1935.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/investigation` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Alois Hofer来问过一次话。问题围绕Franz Moser的病，也围绕Hedwig最近有没有说过什么威胁的话。Hedwig如实回答，Alois Hofer把每个名字都写进了本子。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:146; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 20 -> 30; reputation -0.04; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱); mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1935.01** `kernel-migrate.refugee-aid::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/civic-refugee-shelter` tags: migration, refugee, missing_relative, care, postwar, vienna, urban, crisis

  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; cash -4 (多住一人要补的煤钱和饭钱); condition; flag +sheltered_refugee; knowledge “房东按人头收煤钱，多住一个人要提前说”_

- **1935.02** `r1-fam.friendship-formation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/friendship.bucket` tags: social, friendship, neighbor, water, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig和Klara因为一只水桶吵了一架，谁也不肯先开口。过了半个月，Klara家的孩子病了，Hedwig把自家的退烧药分了一半放在她门口，没有留名字。第二天，水桶被放回了原处，桶底多了一块洗得发白的布。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +reconciled_over_a_water_bucket; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1935.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Katharina Berg在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Katharina Berg敲开Hedwig的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1936.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Hedwig看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004473_

  Alois Hofer把一张修理单放在桌上，上面有Hedwig的签名，还有一个陌生地址。Hedwig从抽屉里找出复写纸的副本，上面只写着一个地址。Alois Hofer把两张纸并排放好，用指甲刮了刮蓝印，说“这张是后补的”。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 10 -> 20; cash -1 (paid for a copy of the repair record); flag +produced_repair_duplicate; knowledge “复写纸的蓝印比钢笔更难改。”; mood -0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

  章程念到“每周四十赫勒”时，Friedrich Hofer 2把帽子摘下来又戴上，说交完房租就剩不下这些。Hedwig把入会费从四克朗降到两克朗，账上立刻短了一截。散会时没有人签字，桌上只剩一张写满数字的包糖纸。
  _effects: cash -1 (互助会的第一本账); mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +mutual_aid_dues_were_too_high; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; knowledge “forty heller a week is more than most can spare”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1938.01** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/broken-tooth` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  夜里十一点，母狮不肯碰桶里的马肉。Hedwig举着煤油灯绕到兽栏后面，看见它右槽牙断了一截，牙龈肿得发亮。Hedwig把肉切碎，又去敲Emil Stein的门；第二天兽医来了，Alois Hofer在晨会上第一次记住Hedwig的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 普拉特动物园夜班队 (other); position 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006024; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:246; skill; reputation 0.03; flag +zoo_night_shift; knowledge “母狮的右槽牙断了，牙龈肿得发亮，夜里不肯吃马肉。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig被叫到学校，老师说Anna Lechner这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Hedwig没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 27 -> 22; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 84 -> 89; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1938.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.read-in-aisle` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Hedwig站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Franz Moser在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Hedwig摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1938.04** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Hedwig当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 59 -> 69; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 5 -> 6; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home); mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005981_

- **1938.04** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Helene Lechner这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 37 -> 40; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006077_

- **1938.06** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Hedwig点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Alois Hofer量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `statistical_infection`
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006060_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Hedwig的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Hedwig家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006354_

- **1939.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Hedwig和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Hedwig把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.029999999999999985; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:274; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Hedwig把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 9 -> 10; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 79 -> 83; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Hedwig把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Hedwig来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 4 -> 5; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  两年过去，Hedwig已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Hedwig也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 40; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 20 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

  Hedwig把Berta Lechner的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Berta Lechner今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Hedwig听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 4 -> 12; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 70 -> 67; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.05** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Hedwig还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 6 -> 8; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006427_

  报纸的社会版把Hedwig家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public true -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 4; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +windfall_went_public_

- **1939.05** `hs18-occupation.piano::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/buy-it-back` tags: occupation, piano, requisition, witness, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  Hedwig把两条舍不得抽的烟塞给Grete Huber，说这琴是替邻居保管的。Grete Huber掂了掂烟，让工人把琴抬回门厅，却把琴凳留下了。Gustav Winter晚上来道谢，Hedwig说别谢，明天要是再来，就说不认识我。
  _effects: cash -3 (two packs of cigarettes for the officer); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:275; flag +bought_back_neighbors_piano; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1939.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Elisabeth Kral嫌Hedwig的床单滴水滴到Elisabeth Kral家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Hedwig下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Hedwig把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Elisabeth Kral那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  Hedwig往芝加哥写了七封信，没有一封回音。Ferdinand Weiss 2在领事馆的窗口翻了半天簿子，说这个名字在入境名单上，后面没有出境记录。Anna Bauer从美国回来探亲，说在屠宰场见过一个同乡，左手少了两根指头；Hedwig把弟弟的照片递过去，Anna Bauer看了很久，说记不清。
  _effects: cash -3 (邮局没有回音); flag +relative_traced_to_chicago; role; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; knowledge “弟弟的名字出现在芝加哥的入境名单上。”_

- **1940.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/counter-motion` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  Hedwig没有等投票，而是让Franz Lechner把委员会过去三年的差旅账目复印了十份。会议开始前，Josef Winter的动议被一份“程序审查”取代；审查没有找到Hedwig的问题，却找到了两次重复报销。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; flag +rival_audited; knowledge “委员会的差旅账目里有两次重复报销。”; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

  风从教堂的拱廊里灌过来，队伍里没人说话，只听见罐子碰罐子的声音。Lena排在前面，领完没走，站在墙根等Hedwig，把自己的面包掰了一半递过去。
  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气)_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-slips-away` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Hedwig把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 28 -> 23; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  第二天的报纸又登了一封匿名信，说Hedwig把一份合同给了亲戚。信里没有证据，但足够让两个原本答应到场的人临时出差。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 40; state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 54; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Josef Winter没有接话，先从抽屉里撕下一角纸。Hedwig把名字、住址和手艺一样样说完，工头边写边应，写完把纸压回抽屉，说月底来问。

- **1940.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Anton Stein 2点了牛奶咖啡，Hedwig要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

  _effects: cash 4 (蜡像馆的讲解酬劳); state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:294; reputation 0.03; flag +wax_museum_inventor; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/carried-out` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  最后一次会议没有让Hedwig发言。保安把纸箱搬到门口，箱子里有半瓶墨水、一只坏掉的台灯和没写完的年度报告。Grete Huber跟在后面，手里拿着新的门锁；走廊尽头，秘书已经把名牌翻了过去。
  _effects: position_end 档案助理 @org-die-fackel; reputation -0.06; condition; flag +carried_out_of_office; knowledge “名牌在会议结束前就被翻了过去。”_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Hedwig把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 4 -> 6; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 11; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 83 -> 80; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Hedwig忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Hedwig听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 82 -> 74; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Hedwig仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Hedwig学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 46 -> 53; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 2; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000009; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig把Berta Lechner的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Berta Lechner今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Hedwig听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 18 -> 26; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 67 -> 64; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `state_expire:raising-child` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig的「家里有个正在长大的孩子」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:38_

- **1941.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Hedwig去了Berggasse 19, Wohnung und Ordination，坐在后排听完了关于Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung在Berggass的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-org-wednesday-psychological-society-1933 as participant; knowledge “我参加了Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung在Berggasse 19,。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.04** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-trial-work` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  介绍所里那个Rosa Weiss说木牌上的地址多半过时了，不如自己去问。Hedwig走了四十分钟到洗衣房，院子里晾着床单。老板娘让她先熨一件试试，看完说留下吧，明天还这个时辰来。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill_

- **1941.10** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sold-the-share` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Otto Bauer把Hedwig寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Hedwig追到码头时，Otto Bauer已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Hedwig在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱); relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000419:child; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition_

- **1941.10** `state_reaction:bc18-ridiculous-fame/exposure-backfires` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  Otto Bauer把Hedwig并不是那个人的证据寄给报社，还附上自己的姓名。编辑把它们写成一篇“旧友回忆”，读者读到的却是两个人多年的交情；Hedwig看见报纸时，先笑了，随后把那一页折起来，放进抽屉最深处。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 38 -> 46; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 12 -> 18; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000419:child; flag +exposure_backfired; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007598_

- **1941.12** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Hedwig还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007511_

- **1941.12** `r3-war.occupation-scrip::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fined-for-trading` tags: war, occupation, currency, black_market, fraternization, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi Lechner 2说与占领军私下交易要罚款，Hedwig把米缸底下的纸马克全交出去。Grete Huber再来时，Hedwig说“不卖了”；Grete Huber把一包糖放在门槛上，Hedwig没有捡，Mitzi Huber替他收了起来。
  _effects: cash -6 (占领军口袋里的纸马克); mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +fined_for_trading_with_occupier; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1942.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Hedwig的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Hedwig家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 1; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007959_

  报纸的社会版把Hedwig家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public false -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 3; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Ferdinand Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008056_

- **1942.01** `bc18-office.new-director-speech::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cut-the-wrong-budget` tags: office, promotion, speech, bureaucracy, public, absurd, comic, vienna
  Hedwig在训话里说“我们要节约用纸”，第二天，档案室不再领墨水，公文开始用铅笔写；一周后，市议会来要正式副本，整间办公室找不到一张能交出去的文件。Hedwig自己掏钱买了两瓶墨水，Alois Hofer在报销单上写“主任训话的后果”，被Hedwig划掉了。
  _effects: cash -2 (自己掏钱买的两瓶墨水); state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 18 -> 12; reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; condition; flag +cut_the_wrong_supplies; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  报纸的周末副刊又把Hedwig的旧照片翻出来，配上一个更整齐的故事：发明、失败、谦逊的晚年。Hedwig写信去更正，编辑回了一张便条，说读者喜欢现在这个版本。

  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Hedwig把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Hedwig来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.02** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Hedwig用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Helene Weiss 2翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Grete Stein在走廊里问交了多少，Hedwig含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.02** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired-relative` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  名单上添的是Helene Lechner，一个连公文格式都分不清的远房亲戚。Ferdinand Weiss 2把考勤表放在桌上，没有问原因；Hedwig也没有解释，只在签字栏里写得比平时慢。第一个月，Helene Lechner迟到了七次，工资照发。
  _effects: position 档案助理 @zoo:person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006024; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001336:child; flag +hired_relative; knowledge “考勤表上记了七次迟到，工资一次没有扣。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Mitzi Lechner 3一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Mitzi Lechner 3的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1943.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Mitzi Lechner 3抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Hedwig把床单对折，腾出一半，Mitzi Lechner 3用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008129_

- **1943.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sells-the-mourning` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Otto Bauer请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Otto Bauer把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000419:child; flag +sold_mourning_service; condition_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1943.06** `df18-blessing.laundry-water::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-candle-stall` tags: blessing, omen, water, faith, fraud, consolation, religious, eerie
  Lena在门口摆了一只木箱，卖蜡烛和印着水渍的小卡片，说摸一摸就能好。Hedwig看着邻居把零钱收进围裙，没有阻止。几天后，楼道里排起了队；Hedwig从后门进出，怕被人认成那个“显灵”的女人。
  _effects: cash 2 (邻居分给subject的蜡烛钱); reputation 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “邻居把我的水渍变成了卖蜡烛的摊子。”; flag +df18_water_stain_shrine; condition_

- **1943.12** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Hedwig还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 11 -> 12; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008575_

- **1944.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-accusation-fails` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Maria Bauer在众人面前说Hedwig用诅咒害人。没有人接话，因为上个月Maria Bauer也用同样的话说过另一个人。事情没有闹大，但Hedwig发现，从此每次走进那间屋子，都有人先看Hedwig的手。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000268:child; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:397_

- **1944.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig自己发起了高烧；Maria Bauer第二天照常出门。没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 2 -> 3_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009239_

- **1944.01** `state_expire:hs18-street-witness` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Hedwig的「街面上的目击者」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1944.01** `state_expire:ml18-sudden-fortune` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  Hedwig的「一笔突然到手的钱」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:312_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig把Anna Lechner的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Anna Lechner今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Hedwig听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 43 -> 51; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 91 -> 88; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Hedwig想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1944.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anna Lechner这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 51 -> 54; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009568_

- **1944.06** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/prayer-mark` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, vienna
  复活节前的夜里，Hedwig在城郊的路边十字架前跪了很久。第二天清早，左手掌心浮出一条细红线，像被针划过，却不疼。Hedwig没有告诉任何人，只把手套戴得更紧；可每次点蜡烛，那条线都比昨天更清楚一点。
  _effects: cash -1 (买蜡烛和灯油); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:399; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_prayer; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1944.07** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cover-up` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Hedwig请Grete Huber去咖啡馆，把一只没有标记的信封留在桌上。Grete Huber没有碰信封，只把报告里“重复报销”改成“记账口径不一”；作为交换，Hedwig签了Grete Huber侄子的实习证明。两个人都没有提那只信封后来去了哪里。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; flag +bribed_auditor; knowledge “审计报告里的“重复报销”被改成了“记账口径不一”，实习证明由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.10** `sm18-object.child-schoolbag::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/asks-where-she-found-it` tags: child, school, object, accusation, misunderstanding, reputation, vienna, urban
  Hedwig没有当场骂Anton 2，只让Grete Stein把顶针先收好，自己蹲下来问是在哪里捡的。Anton 2说在楼梯拐角，旁边还有一只装纽扣的铁盒。Hedwig记得那只铁盒是邻居家的，心里反而更沉。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:398; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004899:child; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; knowledge “孩子说顶针是在楼梯拐角捡的，旁边有一只装纽扣的铁盒”; flag +child_said_she_found_the_thimble; flag +something_is_missing_

- **1944.12** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Hedwig还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 11 -> 12; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009137_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Hedwig躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Hedwig去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

- **1945.01** `curse15-aftermath.the-inquiry::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/money-closes-the-file` tags: witchcraft, investigation, reputation, rumor, legal, aftermath, vienna, urban
  Ferdinand Weiss托人带话，说只要Hedwig出钱修好教堂的侧门，证词就可以当作没有写过。Hedwig把钱数了三遍，最后交出去。侧门修好了，Ferdinand Weiss在门框上刻了一行小字，刻的是Hedwig的名字。
  _effects: cash -3 (修教堂侧门的封口钱); state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 15 -> 0; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +paid_to_close_accusation_

- **1945.01** `state_reaction:sm18-missing-object/a-rumor-hardens` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Hedwig在院子里听见两个人在说“那家的东西”，话头在看见Hedwig时停住。晚上，Hedwig把首饰盒从五斗柜挪到床边，又觉得这样更像在防谁。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 30 -> 40; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009819_

- **1945.01** `state_reaction:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-public` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  Hedwig在人群里挽了一下袖子，第二天就有人来问那块印子是不是“会动的”。Hedwig说不会，问的人却已经把自己的解释说完了。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 10 -> 18; mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009819_

- **1945.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Hedwig当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 87 -> 97; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009845_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Hedwig把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 97 -> 100_

- **1945.01** `state_expire:raising-child` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig的「家里有个正在长大的孩子」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:34_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 20 -> 30; reputation -0.03999999999999998; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱)_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Hedwig把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Hedwig忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 40 -> 48; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 40 -> 38_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Hedwig在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Hedwig低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 18 -> 28; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 0 -> 6; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed blessing -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.08** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Hedwig把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Luise Weiss站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Hedwig手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000124:child; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010144_

- **1946.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/resigned` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Huber在门口堵了三天，第四天把Hedwig的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Hedwig把办公室的钥匙交给Alois Hofer，没有带走任何文件。
  _effects: position_end 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006024; reputation -0.08000000000000002; flag +resigned_over_scandal; knowledge “辞职信被全文刊登，接任者当天就搬进了办公室。”_

  又过了一年，Hedwig学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Hedwig的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 28 -> 22; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 6 -> 2; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/wages-arrive` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Hedwig没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 4 -> 5; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 65 -> 63; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.08** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Hedwig下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: skill_

- **1946.09** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: flag +curse_dismissed_as_fever; condition; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 7 -> 8; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 60 -> 58; mood 0.010000000000000009_
