- **1947.01** `r13-civ.new-district-lodging::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/key-money-demanded` tags: social, housing, lodging, landlady, district, vienna, urban, postwar
  Helene Kral收了房租，又说得再付四克朗的“钥匙钱”，否则前一个房客的家具不搬走。Hedwig在门口站了一会儿，还是数了钱。进屋后才发现，床底下塞着前房客的一只破靴子。
  _effects: cash -10 (新城区门牌下的招租纸); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +lodging_key_money_paid; knowledge “the previous lodger’s furniture was held for key money”_

- **1947.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Hedwig说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Katharina Hofer一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000990:child_

- **1947.07** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Hedwig还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 1 -> 3_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011005_

- **1947.11** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Hedwig上楼敲了Gustav Weiss的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Gustav Weiss把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Hedwig能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1947.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Hedwig在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Josef Weiss从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.03 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1948.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef Weiss嫌Hedwig的床单滴水滴到Josef Weiss家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Hedwig下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Hedwig把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Josef Weiss那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010971_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Hedwig一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Luise 2送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002172:child_

- **1948.01** `df18-curse.name-in-the-door::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/name-and-date` tags: curse, name, door, omen, neighbor, dark, supernatural, vienna
  Hedwig在自家门框上看见一行新刻的字，是自己的名字，下面还有当天的日期。刀痕很浅，像是用钥匙划的。Luise Hofer说不是他，Hedwig也没有再问。那天夜里，Hedwig把门闩插了两遍，听见楼梯上有脚步声停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “门框上出现了我的名字和当天的日期。”; flag +df18_name_carved_on_door_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/a-receipt-in-the-lining` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  半年过去，Hedwig又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 34 -> 40; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 69 -> 67; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/kept-small` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  又过了一年，Hedwig学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Hedwig的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 32 -> 26; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 8 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Hedwig把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Hedwig来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/back-to-work-too-soon` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，她把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Hedwig又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon_

- **1948.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/wages-arrive` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Hedwig没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 8 -> 9; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 58 -> 56; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011877_

- **1948.04** `r1-cul.faith-crisis::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/stopped-communion` tags: faith, doubt, confession, church, grief, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig停止领圣餐，把念珠收进抽屉。Otto Bauer来过两次，第一次带汤，第二次只坐了一会儿。第三周，Katharina Kral邀Hedwig去听一场葬礼上的管风琴；Hedwig站在教堂最后面，没有跪下，却把整首曲子听完了。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; condition; flag +stopped_taking_communion; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; knowledge “the funeral organist played the whole piece without a break”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1948.05** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Hedwig的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Hedwig家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011373_

- **1948.08** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/turned-back` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, vienna
  Josef Winter把材料推回来，说出生纸缺了教区印章。Hedwig解释自己是从乡下赶来的，对方只指了指墙上的告示；排在后头的Anton Lechner递来半块面包，两个人坐在候车室里等下一班回去的车，谁也没有先说话。
  _effects: cash -2 (the return ticket and a wasted day); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001499:child; flag +mb17_turned_back_at_border; knowledge “边境窗口只承认带教区印章的出生证明。”_

- **1949.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/quiet-mercy` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, postwar, reform
  Katharina Berg病好以后来敲过门。两个人隔着门槛站了很久。Hedwig没有说原谅，只把一包药放在对方手里。Katharina Berg走后，Hedwig把门关上，背靠着它坐了一会儿。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +curse_mercy_

- **1949.01** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.belt-patch` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Hedwig拆下一条旧皮带的头，蒙在鞋底破口上，用钉子一点点敲进去。第三下时锥子扎透鞋面，扎进左手拇指根，血滴在鞋帮上。她用围裙擦了擦，还是把皮子钉完了，第二天走路时鞋底有块硬响。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.01** `state_expire:sm18-missing-object` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Hedwig的「一件说不清下落的东西」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:398; flag -something_is_missing_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Hedwig在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Hedwig低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 26 -> 36; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 4 -> 10; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Hedwig把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 9 -> 11; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 4 -> 3; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 56 -> 53; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-slips-away` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Hedwig把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 18 -> 13; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Hedwig咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Hedwig手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Klara等在门口，问值不值，Hedwig只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.020000000000000018; skill_

- **1949.08** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Hedwig还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 4; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011982_

- **1950.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refuses-the-money` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Katharina Kral把钱推过来，Hedwig又推回去。Hedwig说自己只是倒霉，不是什么会看事的人。Katharina Kral把钱留在桌上走了，第二天又派人来取。Hedwig把那一夜省下来的蜡烛收进柜子，没有点过。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; flag +refused_curse_work; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `state_expire:df18-uncanny-mark` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  Hedwig的「不祥的印记」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:399; flag +df18_mark_faded_

- **1950.01** `state_expire:ml18-sudden-fortune` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  Hedwig的「一笔突然到手的钱」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:464_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Hedwig当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 53 -> 63; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 3 -> 4; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home); mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013182_

- **1950.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/church-hall` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  两赫勒一碗，赊账用铅笔记在账本边上。Hedwig 把账本挂在门钉上，风吹得纸页翻动，Helene Weiss 2 用围裙擦手，招呼排队的老人先坐。
  _effects: cash -4 (垫付首日面粉和柴火); food; reputation 0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1950.05** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Hedwig走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000009 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  疗养院的护士接过行李，把床号写在小黑板上。Hedwig躺下时听见走廊尽头有人在放唱片，咳嗽没那么紧了。安娜的信第三天到，说花浇过了，房东没来催租。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1951.01** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Hedwig点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Ferdinand Huber量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Hedwig把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 11 -> 13; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 4 -> 3; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 63 -> 60; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1951.01** `r13-work.open-shop::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-vacant-shop/took-the-vacant-lease` tags: commerce, shop, capital, licence, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  战前那家鞋铺没有撑到战后，货架还钉在墙上。Mitzi Kral 2把租金压到战前的七成，只要Hedwig肯自己修门面。Hedwig用旧报纸糊了墙，把“鞋”字改成“杂货”，开张那天，整条街的人都来看新招牌。
  _effects: cash -9 (卷帘门上的第一块木牌); flag +work13_took_a_postwar_vacant_shop; role; employment Kaufmann @ clinic:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71-evt:world-vienna-1912:009202; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125_

- **1951.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Anton Lechner说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Anton Lechner陪着Hedwig去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001499:child; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.06** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/mother-opened-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  回信没有到Hedwig手里。Helene Lechner的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Mitzi Lechner 3隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Helene Lechner再见到Hedwig时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Hedwig站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; mood -0.039999999999999925_

- **1951.08** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Hedwig把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Luise Hofer替Hedwig扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000834:child_

- **1952.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010443_

- **1952.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fear-spreads` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, postwar, reform
  Ferdinand Huber病倒以后，楼里开始有人避开Hedwig。没有人说是诅咒，但每个人都在数Hedwig最近和谁说过话。
  _effects: reputation -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012184_

- **1952.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Hedwig当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 60 -> 70; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 3 -> 4; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home); mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014169_

- **1952.01** `r13-fam.collect-funeral-money::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/enough-collected` tags: task, social, funeral, neighbor, charity, grief, vienna, urban
  Hedwig端着Elisabeth Kral的旧帽子，从一楼走到四楼，一家一家敲门。有人给两枚铜板，有人给一件旧衬衣，还有人家把门开了一条缝又关上。到傍晚，帽子里的钱够买一口薄棺；Berta Berg接过帽子时，先数了数，又把钱倒出来重数了一遍。
  _effects: cash -2 (端着一顶帽子走楼道); flag +collected_funeral_money; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; knowledge “the building raised enough for a plain coffin”_

- **1952.04** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Hedwig端着一只旧锅去敲Friedrich Lechner的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Friedrich Lechner让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Hedwig连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.12** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Otto Berg追出来问怎么不领了，Hedwig说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Hedwig把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; food_

- **1953.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/the-papers-hear-about-it` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  报纸的社会版把Hedwig家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public false -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 4; reputation -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014728_

- **1953.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/sells-the-mourning` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Klara请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Klara把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Hedwig把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 13 -> 15; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 6 -> 5; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 70 -> 67; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1953.01** `r13-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop::factory-canteen-class/overtime-whistle` tags: education, night_class, adult_education, adult_evening_school, work, numeracy, vienna, urban
  Hedwig刚坐下，汽笛就响了，夜班提前半小时开工。Hedwig把没写完的作业塞进工具箱，第二天发现纸被机油浸透，只剩几个数字还认得出来。那批活赶完，Hedwig领到加班费，却把夜校的课本忘在工具箱里整整一周。油墨把页码糊住了。
  _effects: cash 2 (下班铃后的夜校); mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1953.03** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  水龙头滴滴答答地漏，两人都伸手去扶桶沿。Hedwig看见自家未洗的床单还堆着，胸口发闷，把桶让了半掌宽。晾绳上她的衬衣角被风掀起，正挡着邻家的窗户。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Josef Weiss攥着Hedwig的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1953.06** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stairwell-rumor/stairwell-rumor.ask-straight` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Hedwig在楼梯间拦住Otto Lechner 2，问那句话是从哪儿听来的，又趁送活的人上门时当面看清是谁。原来是对面作坊的裁缝来取改好的裤子。Otto Lechner 2听完把话咽回去，第二天洗衣房里没人再提。
  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1953.12** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Hedwig还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 4 -> 6_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014636_

- **1953.12** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Hedwig的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Hedwig家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014654_

  _effects: reputation -0.06999999999999995; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.severity 0.6 -> 0.7; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:743; flag +curse_recoiled_on_holder_

  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Hedwig当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。

  _effects: state_remove ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:681_

- **1954.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.give-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Hedwig只喝了半杯就看见门口站着等位的老主顾，手里转着帽子。Hedwig把报纸叠好放回木架，起身让出窗边的位子，一杯 Melange 三十赫勒，侍者点头致意，没再收第二杯的钱。走出门时雨小了些，Hedwig站在檐下理了理衣领，觉得这半个下午也不算白来。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (一杯 Melange 的价钱); reputation 0.030000000000000027; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.02** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  汤锅冒着热气，Hedwig盛出两勺倒进Gustav Kral带来的罐子里。Gustav Kral说了声谢谢就匆匆上楼。隔天罐子还回来，锅底搁着一小块猪油。
  _effects: food; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1954.07** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Hedwig去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为环城大道写字楼管理处的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role_

- **1954.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.stitched` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Hedwig用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: skill_

- **1955.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014125_

- **1955.01** `df18-curse.name-in-the-door::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/name-and-date` tags: curse, name, door, omen, neighbor, dark, supernatural, vienna
  Hedwig在自家门框上看见一行新刻的字，是自己的名字，下面还有当天的日期。刀痕很浅，像是用钥匙划的。Mitzi Lechner 3说不是他，Hedwig也没有再问。那天夜里，Hedwig把门闩插了两遍，听见楼梯上有脚步声停了一下。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “门框上出现了我的名字和当天的日期。”_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Hedwig把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 6 -> 7; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 75 -> 79; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 35 -> 45; reputation -0.040000000000000036; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱)_

  签名纸在电车上被挤掉一角，最后十个名字不见了。她站在终点站找了一刻钟，只在座位下找到一张被踩脏的纸；Anna Bauer说“算了”，她还是把剩下的名字重新抄了一遍。她把重抄的名单交给Anna Bauer，让对方再核一遍。
  _effects: flag +petition_signatures_lost; reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸和电车票); knowledge “签名纸要夹在硬纸板里，电车挤掉的一角找不回来”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

- **1955.07** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/wages-arrive` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Hedwig没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 15 -> 16; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 77 -> 75; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: cash -4 (劳动法庭的旁听席); condition; flag +labor_court_dismissed_overtime_claim; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; knowledge “the piece-rate contract is being used to deny overtime”; unemployment (lost job while labor claim was pending)_

  Grete Bauer 2来问过一次话。问题围绕Mitzi的病，也围绕Hedwig最近有没有说过什么威胁的话。Hedwig如实回答，Grete Bauer 2把每个名字都写进了本子。
  _effects: state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:830_

  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销)_

  Otto Lechner 2在众人面前说Hedwig用诅咒害人。没有人接话，因为上个月Otto Lechner 2也用同样的话说过另一个人。事情没有闹大，但Hedwig发现，从此每次走进那间屋子，都有人先看Hedwig的手。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:831_

  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig自己发起了高烧；Otto Lechner 2第二天照常出门。没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 3 -> 4_

  那把菠菜根上还带着湿泥，叶子被压得有些蔫。Anna Huber 2先松了手，说你家人口少，拿半把够了。Hedwig把菜装进篮子时，听见摊主开始收秤砣，铁盘碰得叮当响。
  _effects: cash -0.04 (分买最后一把菠菜); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148_

  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。

- **1957.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Hedwig替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Hedwig自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.040000000000000036; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-160_

- **1931.05** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.lost-dog/lost-dog.feed-bread` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, animal, neighbor
  狗的项圈上嵌着一块铜牌，磨得字迹模糊。Luise Lechner蹲在墙根，把面包撕成小块放在石板上，狗先闻了闻她的手指才低头吃。她摸了摸它的后颈，毛底下那根脊骨瘦得硌手。
  _effects: food; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1931.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Luise Lechner看Otto Weiss盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Otto Weiss愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Luise Lechner门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Luise Lechner打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1932.01** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Josef Huber怪Luise Lechner把人都招到楼梯上，木头泡了潮气才裂的。Luise Lechner没还嘴，蹲下去看那道裂缝，指甲刮下一层朽木屑。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; reputation -0.03; mood -0.06_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Luise Lechner被叫到学校，老师说Friedrich 2这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Luise Lechner没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1932.02** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.crooked-sleeves` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Luise Lechner的剪刀钝得咬布，一剪下去袖口走了斜线。她索性把两只袖子都裁短，袖边用灰线锁了一圈。锁边时线太短，接到第三段才够，右手食指被针顶出个红印，改完像件旧式的短褂。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.06** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Luise Lechner眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Josef Weiss隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Luise Lechner把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Marie Huber，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Marie Huber点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: mood 0.03_

- **1933.05** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Luise Lechner把账本摊在灯下，一页一页念出日期和克朗数，念到买纸那笔时有人 From 门口咳嗽。Mitzi Lechner 2低头拨弄围巾穗子，没有替谁说话。桌子上的旧墨水瓶被碰倒，蓝墨慢慢洇进木纹。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; reputation 0.04; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1933.08** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Luise Lechner没有立刻答话，先去敲Maria Kral的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Maria Kral提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Luise Lechner把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1933.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Franz Stein点了牛奶咖啡，Luise Lechner要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle
  Luise Lechner和Hedwig在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Hedwig把煤钱推过来，Luise Lechner接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/keeps-home` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Luise Lechner没有给Helene Lechner报名。家里需要一双能看摊的手，学校也还欠着上一学期的纸笔钱；晚上Helene Lechner把别人的课本借来看，Luise Lechner假装没有看见。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 38 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 32 -> 8; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 65 -> 40; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 6; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled true -> false; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash 1 (the child's small earnings at the stall); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001336:child; flag +child_kept_from_school_

  Luise Lechner看Sigmund Freud盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Sigmund Freud愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Luise Lechner门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Luise Lechner打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。

- **1934.07** `ml18-gambling.card-system::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/double-to-recover` tags: gambling, luck, money, casino, risk, absurd, rural, agriculture
  第四把庄，Luise Lechner把家里的房租也押了上去。庄家翻出九点，闲家只有三点；Hedwig没有哭，只把戒指从手上摘下来放在桌边。Luise Lechner在天亮前走出赌场，口袋里剩下一枚筹码和一张写着地址的借条；街上的扫雪人已经开始工作。
  _effects: cash -40 (rent and savings lost at the card table); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:88; relation rival with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; condition; flag +lost_the_rent_at_cards; knowledge “纸牌没有记忆，三把庄之后闲仍然不是必然。”; mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.01** `ml18-reversal.old-partner-returns::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/he-was-the-scammer` tags: reversal, money, betrayal, business, scam, comeback, vienna, urban
  Gustav Weiss喝到第三杯才承认：当年那批糖厂债券是他自己印的，受害者不止Luise Lechner一个。他从皮包里拿出一张名单和三十克朗，说这是六个人凑出来的退款，剩下的大概追不回来了。Luise Lechner把名单折好，没有问那三十克朗里有没有Gustav Weiss自己的钱。
  _effects: cash 30 (partial refund from the old partner's other victims); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:131; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +old_partner_confessed; knowledge “旧合伙人承认糖厂债券是他自己印的，受害者至少有六个人。”; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.019999999999999997; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:ml18-reversal/reversal-half-year/the-swing-holds` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  半年里没有新的坏消息。Luise Lechner把零钱分成三份：房租、还账、留一点在抽屉里。旧债主没有上门，咖啡馆里的人也不再每见一次就提那件赔本的事；日子没有变好，但至少不再往下滑。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -40 -> -34; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 50 -> 46; cash 1 (small economies and an odd job); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.07** `r3-war.occupied-bells::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/surrendered-copper` tags: war, occupation, requisition, bells, resistance, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Lechner带人把铜锅、门把手和教堂的烛台堆在广场上，Ferdinand Weiss 2逐个过秤。Alois Hofer想把曾祖父的怀表留下，被Luise Lechner按住手；Grete Stein把自己的铜十字架也放进堆里，说“称这个的时候轻一点”。
  _effects: cash -4 (钟楼上的征用令); mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +surrendered_copper_to_occupier; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1935.11** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/license-it-and-keep-the-patent` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  Luise Lechner没有卖断，而是和Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)签了按瓶抽成的许可：每卖出一千瓶，付两克朗。第一年只卖了四千瓶，Luise Lechner拿到八克朗，却保住了专利。Helene Winter在许可书上盖了一个小章，说这种条款以后会值钱；Luise Lechner把章看了一会儿，没敢笑。
  _effects: cash 8 (first royalty payment on the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:132; skill; flag +licensed_patent; mood 0.04999999999999999; flag -sudden_fortune; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.02; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.11** `state_reaction:ml18-reversal/a-second-chance-arrives` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  一封没有署名的信里夹着一张彩票，号码是Luise Lechner上个月随口说过的。信纸是廉价的学生用纸，字迹却写得很慢。Luise Lechner把彩票翻过来，背面只写着一句：这次别再押上房租。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -50 -> -40; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 55 -> 50; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +reversal_second_chance_

  Ferdinand Lechner把账本塞进钢琴后面的夹层，说等风声过去再拿出来。Katharina Bauer第二次来时带了一个记录员和一张搜查令；钢琴被挪开时，琴键响了一串不成调的音。Luise Lechner看见黑皮本翻开的那一页上，已经用红笔圈出了三笔利息。

- **1936.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Luise Lechner就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Helene Weiss 2摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

  补洞的钱又从另一个洞漏出去：煤价涨了，房东换了锁，那个说好下月还钱的人搬走了。Luise Lechner在账本上把同一笔支出记了两次，第二次是因为不相信第一次。夜里听见楼梯响，就以为是债主。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -34 -> -42; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 46 -> 54; cash -2 (coal, a new lock and a missing debtor); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.03** `vienna-history-freud-consultation/ask-for-a-referral` tags: history, psychoanalysis, freud, freud_followup, medical_consultation
  Luise Lechner 说诊费太高，问能否去医院。Sigmund Freud 写了一张转介条，让Luise Lechner 去综合医院找一位同事；他没有承诺免费，也没有留Luise Lechner 多谈。纸条上的字很小，只写了姓名、日期和“门诊”两个字。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-sigmund-freud; historical development:seed-vienna-psychoanalytic-circle-1912 as participant; flag +referred_to_hospital; knowledge “Freud 的私人诊费超出普通家庭预算时，他会写转介条，让病人去综合医院或同行处继续求诊。”; cash -5 (short consultation and tram fare to the hospital)_

  Helene Lechner把行李捆在自行车后座上，说要去城里找活。Luise Lechner往包里塞了两个面包和一双旧袜子，没有送到车站；火车开走以后，Luise Lechner才发现自己一直站在原地数车厢。
  _effects: move place-wien; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome true -> true; flag +child_left_home; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001336:child; cash 1 (one fewer mouth at the table); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/the-logbook-mislaid` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Luise Lechner半个月的夜班钱；Luise Lechner没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 17 -> 1; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 40 -> 30; mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1938.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/credit-own-name` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  市场掌柜把账本翻到空白页，铅笔写上“月底清”，Luise Lechner 把拇指按进印泥，红印盖得有点歪。Mitzi Lechner 3 扛着半袋面粉往回走，袋底漏出的粉在石板路上留下白印。
  _effects: cash -5 (以自家名义赊购面粉); food; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1938.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Luise Lechner说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Ferdinand Weiss一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1938.08** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Luise Lechner和Katharina Hofer讲好一张床按月四克朗，晚饭另算，钥匙只一把。Katharina Hofer把铺盖卷搬进来，靠在墙边，第一天就把厨房的碗顺手洗了。Maria Bauer过来看了一圈，低声说夜里门要栓好，Luise Lechner点头，把每月四克朗记在包糖纸上。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household_

- **1938.11** `p9-history-medical-daily.house-call/carry-child-hospital` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Luise Lechner用毯子裹住Elisabeth Weiss，搀着往综合医院急诊走，走到半路干脆背起来。长椅上挤满人，等到窗户发白，Elisabeth Weiss的烧退了些，Luise Lechner的胳膊却抖得端不住水杯；雪灌进后领，鞋底磨得只剩一层布，这一夜把两个人的人情结结实实绑在了一起。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1938.12** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Luise Lechner听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 30 -> 38; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 17; mood -0.02999999999999997; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005865_

- **1939.01** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Luise Lechner把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1939.01** `r13-fam.care-for-elder::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::elder-care-war/elder-left-behind` tags: task, care, elder, household, health, intergenerational, vienna, urban
  伤兵站只肯留能走动的老人，Anton Weiss被挪到走廊尽头的临时铺位。Luise Lechner每天去送饭，看见老人的床挨着痰盂，枕头下压着自家的门牌。Luise Lechner把门牌收进衣袋，说明年开春就接你回去；老人点点头，没有问明年是哪一年。
  _effects: cash -2 (给床上的老人翻个身); mood -0.16000000000000003; condition; flag +elder_left_in_corridor; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; knowledge “the elder keeps the old door plate under the pillow”_

- **1939.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/locks-changed` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, migration
  第二个星期，Luise Lechner下工回来，门锁已经换了。床架、锅和一只装票据的铁盒堆在楼道里，看门人Hermann Weiss把一张写有“自愿迁出”的纸递过来，说签了就能把东西搬进后院的共用间。Luise Lechner没有签，先把铁盒抱进怀里，手指被箱角划出一道口子。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; cash -9 (cart, storage and a replacement lock); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; role; flag +evicted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1939.04** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Luise Lechner把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Mitzi Stein替Luise Lechner扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

- **1939.04** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.wash-day/wash-day.shirt-in-mud` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, laundry, neighbor, household
  风是从巷口那栋新楼的方向刮过来的，绳子上的被单都鼓成了帆。衬衫落地时，几个搓衣的女人都停了手看。Luise Lechner弯腰去捡，听见身后有人说风大就早点收，别等出了事再怪人。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `fw19-split.borrowed-goat::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/goat-gives-milk` tags: goat, courtyard, ration, sharing, comic, warm, vienna, urban
  隔壁的Hedwig把山羊牵来，说只借三天。Ferdinand Huber蹲在门口挤奶，挤了半桶；Luise Lechner把第一碗送给Hedwig，第二碗留给Ferdinand Huber，自己舔了舔碗边。
  _effects: food; cash 1 (cash +1); state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 40 -> 43; flag +borrowed_goat_milked; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1940.01** `state_expire:oj18-odd-apprenticeship` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  Luise Lechner的「奇职学徒」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:208; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

- **1940.01** `r13-civ.rehearse-play::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/rehearsal-broken-up` tags: culture, theatre, rehearsal, play, ambition, vienna, urban, crisis
  排练到第二幕，房东下来敲门，说楼下的住户嫌吵，再排就涨租。Grete Huber把布景折起来，演员们抱着道具站在楼梯口。Luise Lechner最后走，关灯时看见舞台地板上有一圈粉笔画的走位线。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +rehearsal_broken_up_by_landlord; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1940.03** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Luise Lechner在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Gustav Weiss却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Luise Lechner把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1940.09** `r3-mig.xenophobic-landlord::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/landlord-called-police` tags: migration, housing, hostility, landlord, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Lechner拒绝加租，Alois Hofer第二天带着警察来，说屋里有六个人登记，实际住了九个。Katharina Bauer站在楼梯口，说愿意替Luise Lechner作证只住了四个。警察数了数床垫，把数字写在本子上，房东在旁边补充“他们晚上还烧煤油炉”。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +landlord_reported_overcrowding; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation friend with person-katharina-bauer; knowledge “the landlord counts mattresses for the police”_

- **1940.11** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  凌晨的墙缝透进凉风，Luise Lechner把线头咬断，发现指尖又渗出一颗血珠。她把做好的领子一张张码齐，用布包好压在枕头边，怕孩子翻身压皱。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Luise Lechner记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Mitzi Huber说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Mitzi Huber陪着Luise Lechner去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; mood -0.04000000000000015_

- **1941.01** `r13-fam.help-neighbour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/carried-the-coal` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, care, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Lechner看见Grete Stein在楼梯口歇了三回，便把煤筐接过来，一筐一筐抬上三楼。最后一趟，Luise Lechner的腰在门框上撞了一下，Grete Stein从柜里摸出一小瓶李子酒，硬倒了一盅。
  _effects: food; health -0.03 (把煤筐抬上三楼); flag +carried_neighbour_coal; role_

- **1941.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Luise Lechner在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1941.04** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Friedrich Stein在午休时把本子摊在Luise Lechner的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Luise Lechner从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Friedrich Stein当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1941.06** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Luise Lechner正要把空床许给Helene Lechner，Mitzi Lechner 2却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Luise Lechner回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Helene Lechner，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1942.01** `r13-work.practice-craft::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/joint-fits` tags: work, craft, practice, wood, skill, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Lechner把第四十个榫头敲进卯眼，声音是闷的，不晃。Rosa Weiss 2路过，用拇指按了按接缝，说这回不用胶也能站住。Luise Lechner把废掉的三十九个榫头扫进灶膛，火苗从锯末里窜起来，屋里全是松木的味。
  _effects: cash 2 (第四十个榫头); flag +work13_forty_practice_mortises; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

- **1942.07** `ld18-wedding.private-rite::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/rings-in-a-borrowed-room` tags: love, wedding, marriage, queer, secret, ritual, commitment, secret_intimacy
  婚礼在借来的房间里举行，证人是洗衣房的Franz Lechner。戒指是从当铺买的，誓词写在洗衣单背面；Franz Lechner念到“无论贫穷还是疾病”时，楼下有人敲门，三个人同时屏住呼吸。门开了，是来收煤气费的。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:338; relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001000:child; cash -1 (the pawnshop rings and the gas bill); mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +private_wedding_rite; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1943.01** `ld18-child.discovery::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-child-tells-the-teacher` tags: family, child, secret, queer, discovery, raising_child, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Ferdinand Huber在学校说，家里有两个妈妈。老师把Luise Lechner叫去，问是不是家庭情况“复杂”；第二天，Franz Lechner来接孩子时，门口多了几个家长。Ferdinand Huber牵着Franz Lechner的手，走得很慢，像在等Luise Lechner跟上来。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:354; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002326:child; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 25; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1943.01** `r2-law.pawn-ticket::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/redeemed-before-sale` tags: finance, pawnshop, debt, household, loss, vienna, urban, crisis
  Franz Lechner把当票对着账本看了两遍，说昨天刚过期，今天还能赎，但要加一个月的保管费。Luise Lechner把工钱和借来的钱一起推过柜台，Franz Lechner从后架取下那只银表，表盖上的划痕还在。Lena在门口说，再晚一天就上拍卖台了。
  _effects: cash -12 (过期的当票); flag +redeemed_pawned_watch_before_auction; knowledge “the pawnbroker charges one month’s storage after the ticket ”_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Luise Lechner和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Luise Lechner在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 70 -> 74; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 25 -> 22; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `r13-work.factory-gate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, hiring, factory, queue, cash, vienna, urban, crisis
  Elisabeth Stein把名单卷起来，说今天只要会看图纸的。Luise Lechner在铁门外站到换班，听见里面皮带轮转了一整天。Friedrich Lechner分给Luise Lechner半块黑面包，两人谁也没提明天还来不来；电车票钱花掉了，口袋里的铜板只够买一小袋煤。
  _effects: cash -1 (铁门开了一条缝); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +work13_turned_away_at_factory_gate; knowledge “the factory takes only ten men from a gate queue of over a h”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1944.01** `ld18-future.two-rooms::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-wall-is-too-thin` tags: love, future, unregistered, commitment, ordinary, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  邻居向Katharina Berg抱怨，说半夜总听见墙里有敲击声。Katharina Berg来问，Luise Lechner说是在赶老鼠；Franz Lechner在隔壁憋着笑，等门关上才敲了四下。第二天，Katharina Berg把墙加了一层旧毯子，房租也加了。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 22 -> 32; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 55 -> 50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; reputation -0.03_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Luise Lechner最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Luise Lechner把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Luise Lechner说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 32 -> 38; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 50 -> 47; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Luise Lechner，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Luise Lechner没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 45 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 40 -> 44; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `r13-civ.enter-hospital::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/admitted-third-class` tags: health, hospital, admission, deposit, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Franz Lechner把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Luise Lechner去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Helene Lechner给Luise Lechner一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Luise Lechner把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class_

- **1944.07** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Luise Lechner喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Josef Winter在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1944.08** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-shared-crates` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  木箱里装的是钉子，一车推上斜坡时Luise Lechner在前头拉，Elisabeth Stein在后头推。结账时工头只点了一个名字，Elisabeth Stein的嗓门整条巷子都听得见，最后多给了两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (争执后补到的搬运工钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1944.11** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.buy` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼摊快收了，Luise Lechner挑了一条最小的鳟鱼，摊主顺手抓了把欧芹丢进纸里。黄油在锅里化开，鱼皮卷起来，楼道里都是味。Ferdinand Weiss敲门时，Luise Lechner从鱼尾切下一小块递过去。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1945.01** `ld18-absurd.moral-prize::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/a-medal-for-model-household` tags: absurd, irony, scandal, public, marriage, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  道德协会把一枚铜牌送到Luise Lechner和Franz Lechner的店里，说她们的铺子窗明几净、账目清楚，是模范家庭。Josef Winter握着她们的手，问什么时候请吃喜酒；Luise Lechner说等生意再好一点，Franz Lechner在旁边把抹布拧成了麻花。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; cash 2 (the prize money and the new customers); state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:417; flag +model_household_medal; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1945.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-family-wedding` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  亲戚的婚礼上，Luise Lechner被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Luise Lechner低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 38 -> 48; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009715_

- **1945.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Luise Lechner，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Luise Lechner说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Luise Lechner把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 48 -> 56; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 47 -> 43; reputation -0.020000000000000004_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009715_

- **1945.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-wedding-invitation` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  请柬上写着Luise Lechner的名字，旁边用铅笔注了一句“一个人来也可以”。婚礼上，新娘把手捧花直接递给她，笑着说“下一个该你了”；同桌的人都在等Luise Lechner怎么回答。她把花放在膝上，说这花很香。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 45 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 10 -> 15; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009715_

- **1945.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-moral-society-letter` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  道德协会寄来一封措辞客气的信，请Luise Lechner去“谈谈自己的生活方式”。她把信读完，折好，垫在桌脚下；桌子还是晃。第二天，她照常去上班，只是把头发剪短了一截，短到所有人都能看出她不在乎。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 15 -> 10; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.defiance 35 -> 43; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009715_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Luise Lechner和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Luise Lechner在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 74 -> 78; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 56 -> 53; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.01** `state_expire:ld18-public-desire` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  Luise Lechner的「被全城观看的欲望」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:354; flag -public_desire_active_

- **1945.01** `r13-fam.foster-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::foster-postwar/foster-from-orphanage` tags: task, care, foster, child, household, orphan, vienna, urban
  孤儿院门口排着领孩子的队伍，Alois Hofer把Luise Winter推到Luise Lechner面前，说这孩子夜里不哭，只是不肯吃饭。Luise Lechner蹲下来，把带来的半块糖放在孩子手心；孩子没有吃，先把糖纸抹平，夹进衣袋。那天下午，Luise Lechner在登记簿上按了手印。
  _effects: cash -4 (门口多出来的一双小鞋); flag +fostered_postwar_orphan; role; relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003922:child; knowledge “the child hoards smooth paper and sugar wrappers”; household_

- **1945.05** `oj18-lift.stuck::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/blackout-lift` tags: lift, building, work, night, passenger, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  停电来得毫无预兆，轿厢里只剩一盏应急蜡。Luise Lechner用手动闸把轿厢停在最近的楼层，又用肩膀顶开门；乘客一个个跨出去，最后一个人回头说“你该去当消防员”。Luise Lechner的胳膊酸了三天，管理处的维修单上却只写“正常”。
  _effects: reputation 0.039999999999999994; cash 1 (blackout shift pay); state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:418; flag +lift_operator; flag +blackout_lift; knowledge “停电时轿厢里只剩一盏应急蜡，手动闸要停在最近的楼层。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1945.06** `r1-hc.burglary::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::burglary-crisis-unemployed/burglary-crisis-recognized` tags: home, burglary, theft, pawnshop, neighbor, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Lechner在包袱里看见一件工装，袖口绣着Berta Berg的名字。第二天Berta Berg来还顶针，说孩子病了，实在没法；Luise Lechner收下顶针，把毛毯也给了他。
  _effects: cash -6 (门锁被撬成了麻花); flag +recognized_thief_as_neighbor; role; knowledge “the burglar was the neighbour whose child is ill”_

- **1945.11** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.school-fork/ask-for-reduction` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  办公室的墨水瓶没盖，Luise Lechner闻到一股酸味。Grete Stein把成绩单摊在桌上，Franz Lechner看了一眼说等开学前一周。母亲在桌布底下摸到那两张纸，问有没有准信，Luise Lechner摇了摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +awaiting_school_aid_

- **1946.01** `ld18-raid.private-club::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-owner-pays` tags: queer, raid, police, public, scandal, nightlife, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Alois Hofer在门口和Grete Huber低声说了几句，塞过去一只信封。警察走后，他转头对Luise Lechner说，下个月的酒钱要加一倍。Berta Lechner问要不要换地方；Luise Lechner说先把这个月过完。
  _effects: cash -1 (the raised bar bill after the bribe); state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 53 -> 58; flag +club_owner_paid_the_police_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Luise Lechner最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Luise Lechner把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Luise Lechner说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 66 -> 72; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 39 -> 36; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Luise Lechner是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Luise Lechner在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 30 -> 26; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 53 -> 55; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Luise Lechner的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Luise Lechner想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 19; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 65 -> 55; mood -0.019999999999999907_

- **1946.01** `r13-fam.confront-rumour::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rumor-stopped` tags: task, social, rumor, reputation, conflict, vienna, urban, postwar
  Luise Lechner把湿床单拧干，隔着蒸汽问Ferdinand Weiss 2那句话是从哪儿听来的。Ferdinand Weiss 2说全楼都在传，不止一个。Luise Lechner让对方把原话再说一遍，然后当着Berta Berg的面把日期、工钱和那晚的去处一条条对给对方听。Ferdinand Weiss 2的手在围裙上擦了半天，说也许是听岔了。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; flag +confronted_laundry_rumor; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; knowledge “the rumour started at the market stall, not in the laundry”_

- **1946.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/stay-for-the-rent` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  东西还到一半，两人算了算房租、煤钱和两个人的工钱凑在一起才够。最后钥匙没收回去，外套也留下；这个月能少花一克朗，话说到“以后”两个字就断了，谁都不往下接，炉子今晚还是得两个人一起生。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001000:child; cash 1 (合租省下一份房租)_

- **1946.08** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stairwell-rumor/stairwell-rumor.warn-the-latch` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Luise Lechner没传话，只在二楼门口站了一会儿，提醒Berta Weiss夜里把门闩好，说楼梯间有人爱嚼舌头。Berta Weiss点点头，把门闩推过去，发出很轻的一声。此后送活的人还是照来，闲话却渐渐没人接。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.10** `bc18-inherit.parrot-condition::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/parrot-outlives` tags: inheritance, family, condition, absurd, death, legal, quiet, vienna
  姑妈的遗嘱只有一条：谁替她养那只灰鹦鹉满两年，谁就拿十二克朗。Luise Lechner搬进阁楼，每天煮谷子、换水、用旧报纸垫笼底。鹦鹉学会了学门铃响，也学会了在Luise Lechner咳嗽时叫“吃药”。两年后，公证人Franz Lechner来验鸟，鹦鹉在他帽子上拉了一泡；Luise Lechner拿到钱，先买了一只新笼子。
  _effects: cash 12 (养满两年鹦鹉得到的遗产); skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; condition; flag +kept_the_parrot; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.01** `r15-fam.child-leaves::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/stays-for-now` tags: family, child, leaving, home, child_left_home, vienna, urban, postwar
  Helene Lechner说再等一年，等家里的债还掉一点。Luise Lechner没有劝，只把晚饭多添了一勺；夜里Luise Lechner听见Helene Lechner在隔壁翻书，煤油灯一直亮到后半夜。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 20; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 54; cash -1 (another year of food and lamp oil); mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Luise Lechner和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Luise Lechner在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 78 -> 82; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 72 -> 69; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Luise Lechner，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Luise Lechner没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 26 -> 34; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 15 -> 19; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.church-organ` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Luise Lechner数了数前面攒动的人头，把口袋里的硬币又攥紧了些，转身离开队伍。走到街角的教堂时，管风琴正从敞开的门里传出来，Luise Lechner在最后一排长凳上坐下，听完整段前奏曲，一个赫勒也没花，只跪得膝盖发酸。出门时与Helene Weiss 3道了别，约好下回还来排队。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Luise Lechner摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Berta Lechner第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Luise Lechner只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1947.05** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Luise Lechner只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Helene Weiss 2手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Helene Weiss 2把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Luise Lechner记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1947.08** `ml18-luck.wrong-horse::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-horse-is-disqualified` tags: gambling, luck, money, horse_race, absurd, windfall, vienna, urban
  马冲过终点，Luise Lechner的票已经举到半空；广播里却说骑师过磅轻了半公斤，成绩作废。Alois Hofer把票收回去，在背面盖了一个“无效”的章，又给Luise Lechner倒了一杯李子酒。Berta Lechner说至少酒是免费的；Luise Lechner喝了一口，觉得比输钱还难受。
  _effects: cash -5 (the losing stake on a disqualified horse（其中5克朗记入债务）); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:463; flag +horse_disqualified; knowledge “那匹马第一个冲线，但骑师过磅轻了半公斤。”; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:ml18-reversal/reversal-half-year/another-turn-of-the-screw` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  补洞的钱又从另一个洞漏出去：煤价涨了，房东换了锁，那个说好下月还钱的人搬走了。Luise Lechner在账本上把同一笔支出记了两次，第二次是因为不相信第一次。夜里听见楼梯响，就以为是债主。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -10 -> -18; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 20 -> 28; cash -2 (coal, a new lock and a missing debtor（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.01** `r13-civ.join-charity::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/donated-first` tags: social, charity, membership, poverty, respectability, vienna, urban, postwar
  Friedrich Winter说可以先不交会费，但要先跟着去一趟贫民巷。Luise Lechner提着一篮旧衣服走了一下午，回来时在登记簿上签了名。Friedrich Winter把一枚铜制小徽章别在Luise Lechner的衣领上。
  _effects: cash -3 (慈善会登记簿上的空行（其中3克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +joined_charity_after_donation; role_

- **1948.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Josef Winter把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Luise Lechner去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中3.3克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1948.03** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Luise Lechner对Katharina Weiss说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Katharina Weiss把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Luise Lechner的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003071:child_

- **1948.10** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-cart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Luise Lechner把铁盒抱在怀里，其余东西都交给Lena和车夫。楼道里挡路的自行车被挪到墙边，锅沿碰上车挡板，咣的一声。新住处门前没有台阶，省得再抬。
  _effects: cash -6 (雇马车搬家具一趟的工钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); residence_

- **1948.11** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  交完活回来，Luise Lechner把空篮挂在门后，盯着篮底看了一会儿。Katharina Weiss端来一碗豆子，说自家孩子吃不完。Luise Lechner接过来，没说那两件歪领子的事。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1949.01** `ld18-journalist.interview::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-story-is-bought` tags: press, interview, public_desire, scandal, money, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Marie Huber把一沓钞票放在桌上，说要写“维也纳最勇敢的爱情故事”，但名字要改，旅馆要删。Luise Lechner数了数钱，够付半年房租；Franz Lechner在旁边说不要卖，Luise Lechner说先活下去，再谈别的。
  _effects: cash 3 (the newspaper paid for a sanitised love story); state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 57 -> 72; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 30 -> 40; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 19 -> 24; relation friend with person-franz-lechner; flag +sold_the_story_

- **1949.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Luise Lechner，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Luise Lechner说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Luise Lechner把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 66 -> 74; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 36 -> 32; reputation -0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012059_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Luise Lechner和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Luise Lechner在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 86 -> 90; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 74 -> 71; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-price-for-silence` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  有人把一张账单寄到Luise Lechner家，背面写着“封口费”，数目正好是她一个月的工钱。Luise Lechner把钱数了两遍，最后还是装进信封；第二天，街上又有了新版本，比上一个更贵，也更难听。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 40 -> 52; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 5 -> 0; cash -2 (the first payment to a blackmailer); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.01** `state_expire:ml18-reversal` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  Luise Lechner的「运势倒转」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:463; flag -fortune_reversed_

- **1949.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.church-organ` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Luise Lechner数了数前面攒动的人头，把口袋里的硬币又攥紧了些，转身离开队伍。走到街角的教堂时，管风琴正从敞开的门里传出来，Luise Lechner在最后一排长凳上坐下，听完整段前奏曲，一个赫勒也没花，只跪得膝盖发酸。出门时与Otto Lechner 2道了别，约好下回还来排队。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.04** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/accepts-the-file` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  户籍官Grete Berg 2为了结掉一桩陈年失踪案，在档案上给Luise Lechner盖了“推定死亡”。三天后，Luise Lechner收到一张慰问卡和一本作废的配给簿；同一天，追了半年的债主忽然不再上门。旧货商听说后，花两克朗买走一份抄本，说要给一个躲债的朋友看看格式。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖给旧货商的死亡证明抄本); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:509; reputation -0.009999999999999995; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144; flag +declared_dead_while_alive; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144; flag +bureaucratic_ghost_

- **1949.07** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Luise Lechner跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Helene Kral探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1949.10** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-witness-stays-quiet` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Berta Lechner在办事处说了Luise Lechner一整个下午的坏话，从欠债说到那场没人看见的火灾。旁边的人没有替Luise Lechner说一句话。几天后，Luise Lechner发现自己的名字从互助会的名单上被划掉了，Berta Lechner只说“我也要养家”。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001000:child; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

- **1950.01** `bc18-ghost.double-tax::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/expose-the-merge` tags: bureaucracy, tax, identity, paperwork, legal, absurd, money, vienna
  Luise Lechner把两张税单寄给了市议会，又抄了一份给《新自由报》。记者查出，两个窗口共用一个旧索引，谁也没核对死亡日期。Gustav Winter被停职两周，Luise Lechner拿到一克朗的“纠错酬金”。回家路上，Luise Lechner想，这大概是死人身份带来的第一笔正经收入。
  _effects: cash 1 (市议会给的纠错酬金); reputation 0.04000000000000001; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 20 -> 30; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144; condition; flag +exposed_double_tax_file; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  办公室有人问Luise Lechner，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Luise Lechner说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Luise Lechner把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 71 -> 79; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 32 -> 28; reputation -0.01999999999999999_
