  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Luise Lechner最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Luise Lechner把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Luise Lechner说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 79 -> 85; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 28 -> 25; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `state_expire:ld18-public-desire` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  Luise Lechner的「被全城观看的欲望」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:417_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Luise Lechner的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Luise Lechner收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Luise Lechner先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 25 -> 32; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 30 -> 40; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `r13-fam.seek-shelter::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/bed-found` tags: task, health, shelter, homelessness, night, charity, vienna, urban
  Marie Huber把Luise Lechner的名字写进本子，发了一张薄毯和一碗热汤。床铺是用木板搭的，翻个身就响；隔壁的女人一直咳嗽，Luise Lechner却睡得很沉。天亮时，Marie Huber说只能住三夜，第四夜要去市政厅重新登记。
  _effects: cash -1 (收容所门口的队伍); condition; flag +slept_in_night_shelter; role; knowledge “the night shelter allows only three nights without re-regist”; move place-wien_

- **1950.02** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/child-at-the-bear-pit` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  一个男孩翻过熊池的矮栏去捡帽子。Luise Lechner没有喊，怕惊动母熊，只提着装苹果的桶绕到下风处，把桶敲得咚咚响。母熊转身时，孩子被Katharina Hofer一把拽了上来；Luise Lechner的腿在后来的很多年里还会在梦里发软。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000990:child; cash 2 (reward from the child's parents); state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:565; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +zoo_night_shift; flag +saved_child_at_zoo; knowledge “母熊被苹果桶引开时，孩子已经挂在{keeper}的胳膊上了。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1950.05** `r13-turn.debt-crisis::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-rent-arrears/defaulted` tags: finance, debt, poverty, household, pressure, vienna, urban, postwar
  第七天，Josef Hofer带着两个人在门口等到天黑。Luise Lechner把家里的现金全数出来，还差九克朗。Josef Hofer没有砸门，只把一张法院传票从门缝塞进来，说下次来的是法警。Rosa Weiss在对面把门关得很轻。
  _effects: cash -20 (催款单贴在门框上（其中10克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +debt_default; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150; knowledge “a court summons follows the unpaid debt”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150_

- **1950.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Luise Lechner在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Josef Winter从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1951.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Luise Lechner把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Josef Winter抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Luise Lechner把床单对折，腾出一半，Josef Winter用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012697_

- **1951.01** `bc18-ghost.census-night::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ghost-story` tags: bureaucracy, census, identity, paperwork, absurd, neighborhood, legal, vienna
  普查员把当晚的事讲给了酒馆里的人听，第二天，街上的孩子开始敲Luise Lechner的门，喊“活鬼出来”。Luise Lechner开门，他们就跑；Luise Lechner关门，他们又回来。月底，一个走江湖的戏班找上门，愿意出两克朗请Luise Lechner在幕间站一会儿，不用说话，只要穿白衣服。
  _effects: cash 2 (戏班请人站幕间的酬劳); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 1 -> 4; reputation -0.01999999999999999; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:608; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; flag +local_ghost_story; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Luise Lechner最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Luise Lechner把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Luise Lechner说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 85 -> 91; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 25 -> 22; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Luise Lechner学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Luise Lechner的材料齐全。年底，Luise Lechner从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 32 -> 36; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 35; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Luise Lechner的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Luise Lechner想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 38 -> 22; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 65 -> 55; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1951.01** `r13-fam.adopt-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::adoption-reform/new-law-applied` tags: task, family, adoption, child, law, household, vienna, urban
  新颁布的未成年人保护法允许养父母登记，但办事处的表格还是旧版，没有“养”这一栏。Grete Bauer 2翻出一叠空白纸，让Luise Lechner把情况写在背面，又请科长来签字。Anna Winter在长椅上数墙上的挂钟，数到第三遍时，Grete Bauer 2终于把章盖了下来。
  _effects: cash -6 (登记簿上的新姓氏（其中0.7克朗记入债务）); flag +adoption_under_reform_law; role; relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:009828:child; knowledge “the reform law allows adoption but offices still use old for”; household_

- **1951.02** `r3-crime.poisoning-case::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::one-microscope/ptomaine-in-the-sausage` tags: crime, medical, poison, marriage, suspicion, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Hofer把剩下的香肠切开，闻了闻，说“是肉坏了，不是人坏了”。Grete Bauer 2不肯信，Josef Hofer把显微镜下的片子推过去，说“你要抓就抓那个卖香肠的”。Berta Lechner在病床上笑了一声，Luise Lechner却坐在椅子上哭了。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +poisoning_charge_dropped; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150; knowledge “the sausage was contaminated with ptomaine”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

- **1951.04** `r2-law.smuggling-cigarettes::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/delivery-completed` tags: crime, smuggling, customs, risk, stations, vienna, urban, postwar
  Grete Kral 3把棺材从货车上抬下来，说里面是“给乡下亲戚的遗体”，Luise Lechner在站台边接应。巡夜的手电扫过来时，Grete Kral 3故意把棺材盖滑开一条缝，露出白布；Josef Winter捂着鼻子退开，挥手放行。出了站，两人在煤堆后面拆出二十条卷烟。
  _effects: cash 18 (棺材夹层里的卷烟); condition; flag +smuggled_cigarettes_in_coffin; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135; knowledge “the station guards will not inspect a coffin closely”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135_

- **1951.05** `r1-hc.assault-knife::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/knife-fight-wound` tags: street, assault, knife, violence, police, vienna, urban, postwar
  Ferdinand Lechner从后裤袋里抽出一把短刀，刀刃在门灯下闪了一下。Luise Lechner抓起椅子挡住第一下，第二下划开了小臂；血顺着手腕滴在地上，围观的人这才动起来，Franz Lechner的哨声从街口传过来。
  _effects: cash -10 (舞厅后门的刀（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; knowledge “the knife came out after the first chair was broken”; move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus_

- **1952.01** `oj18-zoo.escape::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hid-the-chimp` tags: zoo, animals, escape, night, blame, work, odd_job, vienna
  马戏班老板Mitzi在普拉特门口拦住Luise Lechner，说愿意出三十克朗买那只黑猩猩，还缺一个懂它脾气的驯养人。Luise Lechner没有答应，也没有报告；夜里，Luise Lechner把卡斯帕藏进废弃的鸟舍，每天多带一份面包。
  _effects: cash -1 (bread for the hidden chimpanzee（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 18 -> 26; state_add oj18-workplace-legend state:world-vienna-1912:649; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +hid_chimp; knowledge “卡斯帕藏在废弃鸟舍里，听见面包纸响就会从梁上下来。”; role; flag +workplace_legend_

- **1952.01** `ml18-luck.strongbox-in-the-wall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/the-notes-are-still-good` tags: money, luck, housing, windfall, inflation, ownership, vienna, urban
  泥瓦匠敲开卧室的旧墙，掉出一只锈死的铁盒；里面是一叠旧纸币、一枚银扣和一张当票。Mitzi闻声上楼，说墙是他家的，盒子也该归他。Luise Lechner把当票翻过来，看见上面写着自己外祖母的名字，两个人都安静了一会儿。
  _effects: cash 45 (old banknotes found in the wall); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:650; relation rival with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; flag +found_strongbox; knowledge “铁盒里的当票写着外祖母的名字，纸币是很多年前的旧版。”; mood 0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `state_transition:ld18-impossible-attachment/papers-become-public` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Luise Lechner从「无法登记的伴侣」转入另一段处境：the pressure made the attachment publicly legible
  _effects: state_transition ld18-impossible-attachment -> known-affair; reputation -0.1; flag +unregistered_attachment_exposed; flag -impossible_attachment_active; flag +known_affair_active_

- **1952.01** `state_expire:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Luise Lechner的「档案里的活鬼」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:509; flag -bureaucratic_ghost_

- **1952.01** `state_expire:bc18-ridiculous-fame` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  Luise Lechner的「荒唐的名声」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:608; flag -ridiculously_famous_

- **1952.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/chronic-cough` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Luise Lechner在诊所走廊的第三张长凳上坐到中午，膝上压着挂号纸和一块湿手帕。Franz Lechner把听诊器贴上来不到半分钟，说是潮气和烟，开了褐色止咳糖浆，又让下周再来。药费从当天的菜钱里扣走，回家时篮子里只剩半棵卷心菜。
  _effects: cash -3 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +clinic_first_visit; knowledge “the clinic doctor called the cough chronic bronchitis”_

- **1952.07** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/sell-the-horses` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, vienna
  Luise Lechner把灵车和两匹马卖给了城西的殡仪馆，拿到的钱正好抵掉欠账，只剩下一盒黑手套和半瓶擦铜油。Josef Winter说这笔遗产算是结清了；Luise Lechner却总觉得，自己像替一个陌生人做了一场没有尸体的买卖。
  _effects: cash 3 (卖掉灵车和马匹的余款); reputation -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; condition; flag +sold_inherited_hearse; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1952.12** `sm18-neighbor.night-light::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/tells-the-concierge` tags: neighbor, secret, night, window, suspicion, everyday, vienna, urban
  Luise Lechner把这件事告诉了门房。第二天，整栋楼都在传Otto Weiss家藏了人，连菜贩都多问了一句。Otto Weiss在院子里遇见Luise Lechner，没有发火，只把手里那袋土豆换到另一只手，像在让路。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; reputation -0.010000000000000009; condition; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +told_the_concierge_about_the_neighbor_

- **1953.01** `ld18-scandal.rumor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/the-story-grows-teeth` tags: scandal, rumor, public, neighbor, desire, reputation, secret_intimacy, vienna
  有人在洗衣房说，Luise Lechner星期三下午进过一家旅馆，出来时头发是湿的。话传到市场，已经变成另一个版本；Maria Bauer说得最细，连房间号都编好了。Luise Lechner没有解释，只把星期三的工票钉在厨房墙上。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:688; reputation -0.08; state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 30 -> 40; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/the-logbook-mislaid` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Luise Lechner半个月的夜班钱；Luise Lechner没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 22 -> 6; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:oj18-workplace-legend/story-year/story-grows-teeth` tags: oj18-workplace-legend, work, legend, odd_job, rumor, identity, night, state
  故事传到第三个街区时，已经多出一只会说话的猫和两具没有头的尸体。Luise Lechner想纠正，发现自己越解释，听的人越兴奋；酒馆老板干脆把最离谱的版本写在黑板上，下面画了一个箭头：本店常客。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.legend 20 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.truth 55 -> 50; state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.toldCount 0 -> 2; flag +legend_exaggerated_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:known-affair/public-year/retold` tags: known-affair, love, scandal, secret, public, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Luise Lechner在市场上被人叫住，对方笑着说“就是那个人”。Luise Lechner也笑了一下，买完土豆就走；回到家才发现，手里的零钱少了一枚。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 40 -> 48; reputation -0.03_

- **1953.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Luise Lechner听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 26 -> 34; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 6 -> 3; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015190_

- **1953.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Luise Lechner把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Mitzi借给Luise Lechner一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Luise Lechner睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中1.5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1953.06** `r3-mig.xenophobic-landlord::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/chalk-cross-and-rent-hike` tags: migration, housing, hostility, landlord, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Kral在Luise Lechner的门框上画了一个白叉，说这一层要“清理”，外国人的房租从下月起加两克朗。Luise Lechner问租约上没写这一条，Berta Kral说租约是旧政府的，现在归他解释。Maria Bauer在隔壁把门开了一条缝，又轻轻关上。
  _effects: cash -4 (门上的粉笔叉（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.04999999999999982; condition; flag +marked_with_chalk_cross; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; knowledge “the landlord marks foreign tenants with a chalk cross”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1954.01** `ld18-journalist.interview::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-story-is-bought` tags: press, interview, public_desire, scandal, money, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Maria Bauer把一沓钞票放在桌上，说要写“维也纳最勇敢的爱情故事”，但名字要改，旅馆要删。Luise Lechner数了数钱，够付半年房租；Ferdinand Weiss 2在旁边说不要卖，Luise Lechner说先活下去，再谈别的。
  _effects: cash 3 (the newspaper paid for a sanitised love story); state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 50 -> 65; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 60 -> 70; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 35 -> 40_

- **1954.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-moral-society-letter` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  道德协会寄来一封措辞客气的信，请Luise Lechner去“谈谈自己的生活方式”。她把信读完，折好，垫在桌脚下；桌子还是晃。第二天，她照常去上班，只是把头发剪短了一截，短到所有人都能看出她不在乎。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 5 -> 0; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.defiance 20 -> 28_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015405_

- **1954.01** `state_expire:oj18-odd-apprenticeship` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  Luise Lechner的「奇职学徒」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:565; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

- **1954.01** `state_expire:oj18-workplace-legend` tags: oj18-workplace-legend, work, legend, odd_job, rumor, identity, night, state
  Luise Lechner的「职场传说」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-workplace-legend state:world-vienna-1912:649; role; flag -workplace_legend_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:known-affair/public-year/forgotten` tags: known-affair, love, scandal, secret, public, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Luise Lechner的名字从闲谈里慢慢退了下去。街上有了新的婚礼、新的葬礼和新的丑闻；只有裁缝还记得Luise Lechner改过两次同一件外套的腰身。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 48 -> 43; state_parameter known-affair.grief 4 -> 1; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Luise Lechner，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Luise Lechner没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 70 -> 78; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 40 -> 44; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-last-bottle` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  Luise Lechner跑了四家药房，最后在火车站附近的小店找到最后一瓶奎宁。掌柜先问给谁用，又看了看Luise Lechner的鞋，才把瓶子从柜台底下拿出来；价格是官价的三倍。他把瓶子塞进内袋，路上每隔一会儿摸一下。
  _effects: cash -25 (last bottle of quinine at three times the official price（其中10克朗记入债务）); health 0.039999999999999994 (quinine obtained and taken); condition; role; knowledge “奎宁这类药会藏在柜台底下，先问给谁用，再看买药人的鞋；价格是官价三倍。”_

- **1954.06** `df18-blessing.laundry-water::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/repaint-the-wall` tags: blessing, omen, water, faith, fraud, consolation, religious, eerie
  本堂神父来看过，说只是墙里的水管渗水，叫人把墙重新刷了。水渍被白灰盖住，洗衣房亮了一截。Luise Lechner站在梯子下面，觉得少了点什么，又觉得这样也好。那天晚上，Luise Lechner睡得很早，没有点蜡烛。
  _effects: health 0.01 (洗衣房不再潮湿); knowledge “水渍只是渗水，神父让人把墙刷了。”; flag +df18_water_stain_repainted; condition_

- **1954.07** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/sold-a-secret` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi把Luise Lechner在饭桌上说过的名字和地址卖给了别人。几天后，Luise Lechner发现自己的话出现在一封不该出现的信里。Mitzi没有否认，只说“大家都要活”。
  _effects: flag +betrayed_after_trusting; relation rival with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:736_

- **1954.12** `r2-law.legal-aid::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/denied-for-sewing-machine` tags: law, legal_aid, poverty, court, bureaucracy, vienna, urban, postwar
  Katharina Berg把财产申报表翻到背面，问Luise Lechner家里有没有值钱的东西。Luise Lechner说有一台缝纫机，Katharina Berg在“可变卖资产”一栏打了勾，说这就不算贫困。Luise Lechner问缝纫机卖了拿什么接活，Katharina Berg说那是另一个窗口的问题。
  _effects: cash -2 (法律援助窗口的号码牌); condition; flag +legal_aid_refused_for_owning_sewing_machine; knowledge “a sewing machine counts as disposable capital for legal aid”_

- **1955.01** `df18-omen.crows-on-the-wire::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/feed-the-crows` tags: omen, legal, investigation, crow, court, eerie, superstition, vienna
  Luise Lechner把口袋里的面包屑撒在窗台上。乌鸦飞下来啄食，Gustav Winter在窗内看见，笑了一声，说这案子要是也有这么好喂就好了。几天后，Gustav Winter把一份旧记录的抄件塞给Luise Lechner，说别问是谁给的。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 15 -> 10; reputation 0.020000000000000004; knowledge “法院里的官员给了我一份旧记录抄件。”; flag +df18_clerk_gave_copy; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1955.01** `df18-blessing.laundry-water::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/sit-with-the-stain` tags: blessing, omen, water, faith, fraud, consolation, religious, eerie
  洗衣房的墙上有一块水渍，形状像一个披着斗篷的女人。Luise Lechner第一次看见时，正好腰疼得直不起来；她点了一根蜡烛，坐了十分钟，疼没有消失，但呼吸慢了下来。Helene Lechner说那是圣母，Luise Lechner没有纠正，也没有把水渍擦掉。
  _effects: cash -1 (洗衣房里的一根蜡烛); health 0.01 (坐着休息了一会儿); mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “洗衣房的水渍像一个披斗篷的女人，我在它面前坐了一会儿。”; flag +df18_water_stain_consolation_

- **1955.01** `state_expire:known-affair` tags: known-affair, love, scandal, secret, public, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Luise Lechner的「已经公开的私情」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove known-affair state:world-vienna-1912:673; flag -known_affair_active_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Luise Lechner，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Luise Lechner没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 78 -> 86; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 44 -> 48; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Luise Lechner谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 10 -> 25; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1955.01** `r13-work.deliver-goods::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/delivered-on-time` tags: commerce, delivery, transport, weather, bribe, vienna, urban, postwar
  Luise Lechner把十二只木箱码在板车上，用麻绳捆了两道，轮子碾过石子路时，箱角互相磕着响。走到第三道街口，车轴响了一声，Rosa Weiss蹲下去紧了紧销子。货送到时比约定早半个钟头，店主验完封条，在送货单上画了押。
  _effects: cash 6 (板车上的十二箱); flag +work13_delivered_goods_on_time; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003546:child_

- **1955.03** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); flag +watch_pawned_

- **1955.12** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/took-the-room` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, vienna, urban, postwar
  Helene Lechner趁Luise Lechner不在，把租约上的名字改成了自己。Luise Lechner回家时，门口多了一只不属于自己的箱子；Helene Lechner站在门里，说房东已经同意。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001336:child; flag +home_taken_by_betrayal; state_add grief state:world-vienna-1912:781_

- **1956.01** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/hide-the-ledger` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Grete Lechner 3把账本塞进钢琴后面的夹层，说等风声过去再拿出来。Ferdinand Weiss 2第二次来时带了一个记录员和一张搜查令；钢琴被挪开时，琴键响了一串不成调的音。Luise Lechner看见黑皮本翻开的那一页上，已经用红笔圈出了三笔利息。
  _effects: cash -10 (the accountant's fee for hiding the ledger（其中10克朗记入债务）); state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:824; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; knowledge “黑皮本上被红笔圈出的三笔利息都没有报税。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Luise Lechner是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Luise Lechner在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 86 -> 82; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 65 -> 67; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1956.01** `state_expire:under-investigation` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick, state:under-investigation
  Luise Lechner的「被调查」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:736_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/overwhelmed` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Luise Lechner在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; state_parameter grief.years 0 -> 1_

- **1956.01** `r13-civ.donate-clothes::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/coat-donated` tags: social, charity, clothing, poverty, household, vienna, urban, postwar
  Luise Lechner把父亲留下的厚呢外套从箱底翻出来，掸掉樟脑味，叠好交给Elisabeth Weiss。Elisabeth Weiss摸了摸袖口，说这料子还能穿三年，就在收据上写了“男式冬衣一件”。回家时，箱子空出一角。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +donated_winter_coat; knowledge “the coat was logged as a man’s winter coat”_

- **1956.03** `bc18-fame.wrong-bust::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/plaque-stays` tags: fame, mistaken_identity, museum, public, invention, absurd, status, humiliation
  市立蜡像馆新添了一间“发明家厅”。Grete Bauer 2把Luise Lechner请到绿绒椅子上，说照片是从旧报纸上剪的，名字也核对过三遍。揭幕那天，Luise Lechner看见自己的蜡像握着一根安全火柴，铭牌上写着一个从未属于Luise Lechner的专利号；掌声很整齐，Grete Bauer 2把下一场讲解的酬劳塞进口袋，动作像在递一块糖。
  _effects: cash 4 (蜡像馆的讲解酬劳); state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:825; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; flag +wax_museum_inventor; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Luise Lechner在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Elisabeth Weiss从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000243:child_

- **1956.07** `r3-mig.boarding-house::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hot-bed-rented` tags: migration, housing, poverty, lodging, neighbor, vienna, urban, postwar
  Helene Lechner收了三克朗，把Luise Lechner领到一间放了四张铁床的屋子，说这张床白天归别人，晚上八点以后归Luise Lechner。床单还是热的，枕头上有别人的头发。Otto Weiss正在穿鞋，说“你睡的时候我上工，别动我的饭盒”。
  _effects: cash -3 (床位按八小时轮班（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +rented_hot_bed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; knowledge “the bed is rented in eight-hour shifts”_

- **1957.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Luise Lechner把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Elisabeth Weiss抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Luise Lechner把床单对折，腾出一半，Elisabeth Weiss用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016883_

- **1957.01** `bc18-fame.merchandise::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/carnival-stall` tags: fame, merchandise, business, absurd, legal, public, reputation, vienna
  厂里剩下一箱卖不掉的假胡子，Luise Lechner用三克朗买下，在狂欢节门口摆了个摊。孩子们把胡子别在帽檐上，年轻人买去参加化装舞会；一个老妇人买了两副，说给远行的儿子留着。收摊时，Luise Lechner数出五克朗，比本金多了两克朗，还剩下半箱胡子。
  _effects: cash 3 (狂欢节卖假胡子的利润); skill; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 38 -> 36; role; flag +sold_moustaches_at_carnival; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1957.01** `state_reaction:bc18-ridiculous-fame/reprinted-wrong` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  报纸的周末副刊又把Luise Lechner的旧照片翻出来，配上一个更整齐的故事：发明、失败、谦逊的晚年。Luise Lechner写信去更正，编辑回了一张便条，说读者喜欢现在这个版本。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 36 -> 42; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.absurdity 72 -> 77; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 12 -> 8; flag +story_reprinted_wrong; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017587_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Luise Lechner，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Luise Lechner没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 82 -> 90; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 48 -> 52; reputation -0.03_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/carried` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Luise Lechner又过了一年。那个人的名字不再每天出现，但在某些气味和街道上仍会回来。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 1 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `state_expire:under-investigation` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick, state:under-investigation
  Luise Lechner的「被调查」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:824_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Luise Lechner仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Luise Lechner学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 42 -> 49; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 2; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000002; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `r13-fam.repair-friendship::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/cup-refused` tags: task, social, friendship, repair, trust, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton 2把杯子推回来，说摔坏的东西粘得再好也有缝。Luise Lechner在门口站了一会儿，把杯子放进外套口袋，杯沿磕在扣子上，响了一声。回家的路上，Luise Lechner绕开了两人常走的那条街。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +friendship_unrepaired_

- **1957.06** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Luise Lechner在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Marie Huber却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Luise Lechner把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.06** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.factory-gate/gate-queue-clash` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, factory, hiring
  招工纸上的工钱写得明白，一天三克朗八十赫勒。Luise Lechner怕再等下去名额没了，从人缝里挤到前面，后面的Helene Weiss 3一把拽住袖子。门房抬头看了看乱糟糟的队伍，把玻璃窗拉上了。
  _effects: reputation -0.03_

- **1957.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Luise Lechner把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Helene Hofer借给Luise Lechner一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Luise Lechner睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); mood -0.020000000000000018_
